      { text: "\u062E\u0637\u0651\u064A\u0646 \u0644\u0623\u0646\u0646\u064A \u0623\u0631\u0649 \u062E\u0637 \u0627\u0644\u0628\u062F\u0627\u064A\u0629 \u0648\u062E\u0637 \u0627\u0644\u0646\u0647\u0627\u064A\u0629", right: false, feedback: "\u062E\u0637 \u0627\u0644\u0628\u062F\u0627\u064A\u0629 \u0647\u0648 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u0641\u0631 (\u0644\u0645 \u062A\u0642\u0641\u0632 \u0628\u0639\u062F). \u0627\u0644\u0639\u062F\u062F \u064A\u0645\u062B\u0644 \u0639\u062F\u062F \u0627\u0644\u062E\u0637\u0648\u0627\u062A \u0623\u0648 \u0627\u0644\u0642\u0641\u0632\u0627\u062A \u0627\u0644\u062A\u064A \u062A\u062D\u0631\u0643\u062A\u0647\u0627!" }
    ],
    rule: "\u0639\u0644\u0649 \u062E\u0637 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0639\u062F\u0627\u062F: \u0646\u0639\u062F\u0651 \u0627\u0644\u0642\u0641\u0632\u0627\u062A \u0648\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0633\u0627\u0641\u0627\u062A \u0627\u0628\u062A\u062F\u0627\u0621\u064B \u0645\u0646 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u0641\u0631\u060C \u0648\u0644\u0627 \u0646\u0639\u062F\u0651 \u0627\u0644\u062E\u0637\u0648\u0637."
  }
};
var SocraticTutor = {
  getHelp(tag, questionText, correctAnswer) {
    const data = SOCRATIC_DATA[tag];
    if (data) {
      return {
        tag,
        title: data.title,
        thought: data.tigerThought,
        q: data.question,
        opts: data.options,
        rule: data.rule
      };
    }
    return {
      tag: tag || "general",
      title: "\u0627\u0644\u062A\u0641\u0643\u064A\u0631 \u062E\u0637\u0648\u0629 \u0628\u062E\u0637\u0648\u0629",
      thought: "\u0644\u0627 \u0628\u0623\u0633 \u064A\u0627 \u0628\u0637\u0644\u060C \u0627\u0644\u062E\u0637\u0623 \u062E\u0637\u0648\u0629 \u0623\u0648\u0644\u0649 \u0646\u062D\u0648 \u0627\u0644\u0641\u0647\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u062D\u0642\u064A\u0642\u064A! \u062A\u0639\u0627\u0644 \u0646\u0628\u0633\u0651\u0637 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0633\u0623\u0644\u0629 \u0645\u0639\u064B\u0627:",
      q: "\u0645\u0627 \u0647\u064A \u0623\u0648\u0644 \u062E\u0637\u0648\u0629 \u064A\u062C\u0628 \u0623\u0646 \u0646\u062A\u0623\u0643\u062F \u0645\u0646\u0647\u0627 \u0641\u064A \u0647\u0630\u0647 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0633\u0623\u0644\u0629\u061F",
      options: [
        { text: "\u0642\u0631\u0627\u0621\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0631\u0642\u0627\u0645 \u0628\u0647\u062F\u0648\u0621 \u0648\u062A\u062D\u062F\u064A\u062F \u0646\u0648\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0645\u0644\u064A\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0637\u0644\u0648\u0628\u0629", right: true, feedback: "\u0631\u0627\u0626\u0639! \u062D\u062F\u062F \u0645\u0627 \u064A\u062D\u062F\u062B \u0641\u064A \u0627\u0644\u0642\u0635\u0629 \u0623\u0648\u0644\u0627\u064B: \u0647\u0644 \u0647\u0648 \u0636\u0645 (+) \u0623\u0645 \u0641\u0631\u0642 (\u2212) \u0623\u0645 \u062A\u0643\u0631\u0627\u0631 (\xD7) \u0623\u0645 \u062A\u0648\u0632\u064A\u0639 (\xF7)\u061F" },
        { text: "\u0643\u062A\u0627\u0628\u0629 \u0623\u064A \u0631\u0642\u0645 \u0628\u0633\u0631\u0639\u0629", right: false, feedback: "\u0627\u0644\u0631\u064A\u0627\u0636\u064A\u0627\u062A \u062A\u062D\u0628 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0623\u0646\u064A! \u062E\u0631\u0628\u0634 \u062E\u0637\u0648\u0627\u062A\u0643 \u0641\u064A \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0633\u0648\u062F\u0629 \u062E\u0637\u0648\u0629 \u0628\u062E\u0637\u0648\u0629 \u0644\u062A\u0631\u0627\u0647\u0627 \u0648\u0627\u0636\u062D\u0629." }

// public/js/scratchpad.js
var Scratchpad = class {
  constructor(containerId = "scratchpad-container") {
    this.containerId = containerId;
    this.canvas = null;
    this.ctx = null;
    this.drawing = false;
    this.color = "#1d2740";
    this.size = 3.5;
    this.mode = "pen";
    this.hasGrid = true;
    this.strokes = [];
    this.currentStroke = null;
  }
  renderUI() {
    return `
      <div class="scratch-wrap" id="${this.containerId}">
        <div class="scratch-bar">
          <div class="scratch-tools">
            <button type="button" class="s-btn active" data-s="pen:#1d2740" title="\u0642\u0644\u0645 \u0623\u0632\u0631\u0642">\u270F\uFE0F \u0623\u0632\u0631\u0642</button>
            <button type="button" class="s-btn" data-s="pen:#e65100" title="\u0642\u0644\u0645 \u0628\u0631\u062A\u0642\u0627\u0644\u064A">\u270F\uFE0F \u0628\u0631\u062A\u0642\u0627\u0644\u064A</button>
            <button type="button" class="s-btn" data-s="pen:#2e7d32" title="\u0642\u0644\u0645 \u0623\u062E\u0636\u0631">\u270F\uFE0F \u0623\u062E\u0636\u0631</button>
            <button type="button" class="s-btn" data-s="eraser" title="\u0645\u0645\u062D\u0627\u0629">\u{1F9FD} \u0645\u0645\u062D\u0627\u0629</button>
            <button type="button" class="s-btn" data-s="grid" title="\u062A\u0628\u062F\u064A\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0634\u0628\u0643\u0629">\u{1F532} \u0634\u0628\u0643\u0629</button>
            <button type="button" class="s-btn" data-s="stamp:tens" title="\u0625\u0636\u0627\u0641\u0629 \u0639\u0645\u0648\u062F \u0639\u0634\u0631\u0627\u062A">+ \u0639\u0634\u0631\u0627\u062A (10)</button>
            <button type="button" class="s-btn" data-s="stamp:ones" title="\u0625\u0636\u0627\u0641\u0629 \u0645\u0643\u0639\u0628 \u0622\u062D\u0627\u062F">+ \u0622\u062D\u0627\u062F (1)</button>
            <button type="button" class="s-btn s-del" data-s="clear" title="\u0645\u0633\u062D \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0633\u0648\u062F\u0629">\u{1F5D1}\uFE0F \u0645\u0633\u062D</button>
          </div>
          <button type="button" class="s-btn s-close" data-s="close" title="\u0625\u063A\u0644\u0627\u0642 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0633\u0648\u062F\u0629">\u2715 \u0625\u063A\u0644\u0627\u0642</button>
        </div>
        <div class="scratch-canvas-box">
          <canvas class="scratch-canvas" touch-action="none"></canvas>
        </div>
        <div class="scratch-tip"><small>\u{1F4A1} \u0627\u0631\u0633\u0645 \u0648\u062C\u0631\u0651\u0628 \u062E\u0637\u0648\u0627\u062A\u0643 \u0628\u062D\u0631\u064A\u0629 \u0642\u0628\u0644 \u0643\u062A\u0627\u0628\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u062C\u0648\u0627\u0628</small></div>
      </div>
    `;
  }
  init(containerEl) {
    if (!containerEl) return;
    this.canvas = containerEl.querySelector(".scratch-canvas");
    if (!this.canvas) return;
    this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext("2d");
    this.resize();
    window.addEventListener("resize", () => this.resize());
    this.canvas.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => this.start(e));
    this.canvas.addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => this.draw(e));
    this.canvas.addEventListener("pointerup", () => this.end());
    this.canvas.addEventListener("pointercancel", () => this.end());
    containerEl.querySelectorAll("[data-s]").forEach((b) => {
      b.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const act2 = b.dataset.s;
        if (act2.startsWith("pen:")) {
          this.mode = "pen";
          this.color = act2.split(":")[1];
          containerEl.querySelectorAll(".s-btn").forEach((x) => x.classList.remove("active"));
          b.classList.add("active");
        } else if (act2 === "eraser") {
          this.mode = "eraser";
          containerEl.querySelectorAll(".s-btn").forEach((x) => x.classList.remove("active"));
          b.classList.add("active");
        } else if (act2 === "grid") {
          this.hasGrid = !this.hasGrid;
          b.classList.toggle("active", this.hasGrid);
          this.redraw();
        } else if (act2 === "clear") {
          this.strokes = [];
          this.redraw();
        } else if (act2 === "stamp:tens") {
          this.addTenStamp();
        } else if (act2 === "stamp:ones") {
          this.addOneStamp();
        } else if (act2 === "close") {
          containerEl.style.display = "none";
        }
      });
    });
    this.redraw();
  }
  resize() {
    if (!this.canvas) return;
    const rect = this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    const w = Math.max(rect.width || 320, 320);
    const h = 260;
    this.canvas.width = w * dpr;
    this.canvas.height = h * dpr;
    this.ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
    this.w = w;
    this.h = h;
    this.redraw();
  }
  getPos(e) {
    const rect = this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    return {
      x: e.clientX - rect.left,
      y: e.clientY - rect.top
    };
  }
  start(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    this.canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    this.drawing = true;
    const p = this.getPos(e);
    this.currentStroke = {
      mode: this.mode,
      color: this.color,
      size: this.mode === "eraser" ? 18 : this.size,
      pts: [p]
    };
    this.strokes.push(this.currentStroke);
  }
  draw(e) {
    if (!this.drawing || !this.currentStroke) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const p = this.getPos(e);
    this.currentStroke.pts.push(p);
    this.drawSegment(this.currentStroke);
  }
  end() {
    this.drawing = false;
    this.currentStroke = null;
  }
  drawSegment(s) {
    const pts = s.pts;
    if (pts.length < 2) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.lineCap = "round";
    ctx.lineJoin = "round";
    ctx.lineWidth = s.size;
    if (s.mode === "eraser") {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#ffffff";
      ctx.lineWidth = 22;
    } else {
      ctx.strokeStyle = s.color;
    }
    ctx.beginPath();
    const p1 = pts[pts.length - 2];
    const p2 = pts[pts.length - 1];
    ctx.moveTo(p1.x, p1.y);
    ctx.lineTo(p2.x, p2.y);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  redraw() {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, this.w, this.h);
    if (this.hasGrid) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#e1e7f0";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      const step = 24;
      for (let x = 0; x <= this.w; x += step) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(x, 0);
        ctx.lineTo(x, this.h);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      for (let y = 0; y <= this.h; y += step) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, y);
        ctx.lineTo(this.w, y);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }
    this.strokes.forEach((s) => {
      if (s.type === "stamp") {
        this.renderStamp(s);
        return;
      }
      if (!s.pts || s.pts.length < 2) return;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.lineCap = "round";
      ctx.lineJoin = "round";
      ctx.lineWidth = s.mode === "eraser" ? 22 : s.size;
      ctx.strokeStyle = s.mode === "eraser" ? "#ffffff" : s.color;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(s.pts[0].x, s.pts[0].y);
      for (let i = 1; i < s.pts.length; i++) {
        ctx.lineTo(s.pts[i].x, s.pts[i].y);
      }
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    });
  }
  addTenStamp() {
    const x = Math.min(this.w - 40, 20 + this.strokes.length % 6 * 35);
    const y = 30;
    this.strokes.push({ type: "stamp", kind: "tens", x, y });
    this.redraw();
  }
  addOneStamp() {
    const x = Math.min(this.w - 30, 20 + this.strokes.length % 8 * 28);
    const y = 180;
    this.strokes.push({ type: "stamp", kind: "ones", x, y });
    this.redraw();
  }
  renderStamp(s) {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    ctx.save();
    if (s.kind === "tens") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "#fff3e0";
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#e65100";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      const w = 22, h = 130;
      ctx.fillRect(s.x, s.y, w, h);
      ctx.strokeRect(s.x, s.y, w, h);
      for (let i = 1; i < 10; i++) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y + i * h / 10);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + w, s.y + i * h / 10);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
    } else if (s.kind === "ones") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e8f5e9";
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#2e7d32";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      const size = 20;
      ctx.fillRect(s.x, s.y, size, size);
      ctx.strokeRect(s.x, s.y, size, size);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
};

// public/js/engine.js
var N = {};
var r = (a, b) => a + Math.floor(Math.random() * (b - a + 1));
var sh = (a) => a.map((x) => [Math.random(), x]).sort((p, q) => p[0] - q[0]).map((x) => x[1]);
var gcd = (a, b) => b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;
var F = (n, d) => n + "/" + d;
var val = (s) => {
  s = String(s).trim();
  const mx = s.match(/^(\d+)\s+(\d+)\/(\d+)$/);
  if (mx) return +mx[1] + mx[2] / mx[3];
  const p = s.split("/");
  if (p.length === 2 && /^\d+$/.test(p[0]) && /^\d+$/.test(p[1])) return p[0] / p[1];
  return /^-?[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$/.test(s) ? parseFloat(s) : null;
};
var esc = (s) => String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, (c) => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" })[c]);
var fr = (s) => String(s).replace(/[0-9؟]+(?:\s*[+\-−×÷=\/]\s*[0-9؟]+)+/g, (m) => `<span dir="ltr">${m}</span>`).replace(/(\d+) <span dir="ltr">(\d+)\/(\d+)<\/span>/g, '<span dir="ltr" class="mx">$1<span class="f"><i>$2</i><b>$3</b></span></span>').replace(/([0-9؟]+)\/([0-9]+)/g, '<span class="f"><i>$1</i><b>$2</b></span>');
var bar = (n, f, tap) => `<div class="bar${tap ? " tap" : ""}" role="img" aria-label="\u0634\u0631\u064A\u0637 \u0645\u0646 ${n} \u0623\u062C\u0632\u0627\u0621">${Array.from(
  { length: n },
  (_, i) => `<span ${tap ? `data-a="tog" data-k="${i}"` : ""} class="${f(i)}"></span>`
).join("")}</div>`;
var stp = (k, key, mn, mx, lab, step = 1) => `<div class="stp"><button data-a="set" data-k="${key}:-${step}:${mn}:${mx}" aria-label="\u0623\u0646\u0642\u0635 ${lab}">\u2212</button><span>${lab}: <b>${k}</b></span><button data-a="set" data-k="${key}:${step}:${mn}:${mx}" aria-label="\u0632\u0650\u062F ${lab}">+</button></div>`;
var grid = (cols, cells, max = 240) => `<div class="grid" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(${cols},1fr);max-width:${max}px">${cells.map((c) => `<span class="${c}"></span>`).join("")}</div>`;
var g100 = (n, c = "on") => `<div class="grid g10">${Array.from({ length: 100 }, (_, i) => `<span class="${i < n ? c : ""}"></span>`).join("")}</div>`;
var tf = (n, c = "on", faded = 0) => `<div class="tf">${Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => `<span class="${i < n ? i >= n - faded ? "gone" : c : ""}"></span>`).join("")}</div>`;
var nl = (a, b, p, st) => {
  let t = "";
  for (let v = a; v <= b; v += st) t += `<i style="left:${(v - a) / (b - a) * 100}%"></i>`;
  return `<div class="nl" dir="ltr"><div class="ln">${t}<b style="left:${(p - a) / (b - a) * 100}%">\u25BC</b></div><span>${a}</span><span>${b}</span></div>`;
};
var chips = (opts2, cur, key) => `<div class="stp wrap">${opts2.map((o, i) => `<button data-a="set" data-k="${key}:${i - cur}:0:${opts2.length - 1}" class="${i === cur ? "pri" : ""}">${o}</button>`).join("")}</div>`;
Object.assign(N, { r, sh, gcd, F, val, fr, esc, bar, stp, grid, g100, tf, nl, chips });
N.SK = {};
N.C = {};
N.ER = {};
N.def = (k, c) => N.C[k] = c;
N.has = (k) => !!N.C[k];
N.erc = (t) => typeof N.ER[t] === "string" ? N.ER[t].replace(/^قد (لا )?ي/, (m, n) => "\u0631\u0628\u0645\u0627 " + (n || "") + "\u062A") : null;
N.gen = (s, story) => {
  const C = N.C[s];
  for (let tries = 0; tries < 60; tries++) {
    const g = C.gens[r(0, C.gens.length - 1)];
    const x = g(story);
    const ok = [x.o[0]];
    if ("CDE".includes(s)) {
      const pm = String(x.o[0][0]).match(/^(\d+)\/(\d+)$/);
      if (pm && (gcd(+pm[1], +pm[2]) > 1 || +pm[1] >= +pm[2])) continue;
    }
    x.o.slice(1).forEach((o) => {
      const v = val(o[0]);
      if (!ok.some((k) => k[0] === o[0] || v !== null && val(k[0]) === v)) ok.push(o);
    });
    if (ok.length >= 3) {
      x.o = ok;
      x.ans = ok[0][0];
      x.s = s;
      return x;
    }
  }
  throw new Error("generator failed for " + s);
};
N.typable = (q) => !q.pick && /^(\d+|\d+\/\d+|\d+\.\d+|\d+ \d+\/\d+)$/.test(String(q.ans));
N.gradeTyped = (q, input) => {
  const t = String(input).trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  if (!t) return { ok: false, tag: "idk" };
  if (t === String(q.ans)) return { ok: true };
  const v = val(t);
  const exact = q.exact || "SY".includes(q.s);
  if (!exact && v !== null && Math.abs(v - val(q.ans)) < 1e-9) return { ok: true };
  const hit = q.o.find((o) => o[1] !== 0 && (o[0] === t || v !== null && val(o[0]) === v));
  return { ok: false, tag: hit ? hit[1] : "other" };
};

var fw = (n, d) => d == 2 ? n == 1 ? "\u0646\u0650\u0635\u0652\u0641" : n + " \u0645\u0646 2" : FN[d] ? n == 1 ? FN[d][0] : n == 2 ? FN[d][0] + "\u064E\u0627\u0646" : n + " " + FN[d][1] : n + " \u0645\u0646 " + d;
var spk = (h) => String(h).replace(/<span class="f"><i>(.*?)<\/i><b>(.*?)<\/b><\/span>/g, "$1/$2").replace(/<br>|<\/div>|<\/p>/g, ". var VC = null;
N.voice = () => {
  if (VC) return VC;
  if (typeof speechSynthesis === "undefined") return null;
  const v = speechSynthesis.getVoices().filter((x) => /^ar/i.test(x.lang));
  if (!v.length) return VC = null, { lang: "ar-SA", fallback: true };
  const score = (x) => (/female|woman|girl|laila|layla|salma|zira|heam|hana|amira|noura|noora|fatima|sara|arabic.*(female|woman)/i.test(x.name) ? 8 : 0) + (/natural|neural|premium|enhanced|online/i.test(x.name) ? 4 : 0) + (/google|microsoft|apple|maged|zariyah|naayf|shakir/i.test(x.name) ? 2 : 0) + (/ar[-_](EG)/i.test(x.lang) ? 1.5 : 0) + (/^ar/i.test(x.lang) ? 1 : 0) + (x.localService ? 0.5 : 0);
  return VC = v.slice().sort((a, b) => score(b) - score(a))[0];
};
if (typeof speechSynthesis !== "undefined") speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged = () => VC = null;
N.speak = (t) => {
  if (typeof speechSynthesis === "undefined") return false;
  try {
    const v = N.voice();
    if (!v) return false;
    speechSynthesis.cancel();
    const u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(spk(t));
    if (!v.fallback && v.name) u.voice = v;
    u.lang = v.lang || "ar-SA";
    u.volume = 1;
    if (speechSynthesis.paused) speechSynthesis.resume();
    u.rate = 0.95;
    u.pitch = 1.1;
    speechSynthesis.speak(u);
    return true;
  } catch (e) {
    return false;
  }
};
N.hush = () => typeof speechSynthesis !== "undefined" && speechSynthesis.cancel();
N.plain = (el) => {
  const c = el.cloneNode(true);
  c.querySelectorAll("button,svg,.bar,.grid,.nl,.tf,.cols").forEach((x) => x.remove());
  return c.innerHTML;
};
if (typeof window !== "undefined") window.NIMR = N;

// public/js/audio-fx.js
var SoundFX = class {
  constructor() {
    this.ctx = null;
    this.on = () => true;
    this.soft = () => false;
    this.unlocked = false;
  }
  unlock() {
    if (this.unlocked && this.ctx && this.ctx.state === "running") return;
    try {
      const A = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
      if (!A) return;
      if (!this.ctx) {
        this.ctx = new A();
        this.out = this.ctx.createDynamicsCompressor();
        this.out.threshold.value = -10;
        this.out.ratio.value = 4;
        this.master = this.ctx.createGain();
        this.master.gain.value = 1.6;
        this.master.connect(this.out).connect(this.ctx.destination);
      }
      if (this.ctx.state === "suspended") {
        this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {
        });
      }
      const b = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, 1, 22050);
      const s = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
      s.buffer = b;
      s.connect(this.ctx.destination);
      s.start(0);
      this.unlocked = true;
    } catch (e) {
    }
  }
  ready() {
    if (!this.on()) return null;
    this.unlock();
    if (this.ctx && this.ctx.state === "suspended") {
      this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {
      });
    }
    return this.ctx;
  }
  note(f, at, dur, vol = 0.2, type = "triangle", slideTo) {
    try {
      const c = this.ready();
      if (!c) return;
      const t = c.currentTime + at;
      const v = vol * (this.soft() ? 0.45 : 1);
      const o = c.createOscillator();
      const g = c.createGain();
      o.type = type;
      o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f, t);
      if (slideTo) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(20, slideTo), t + dur);
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(v, t + 0.015);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + dur);
      o.connect(g).connect(this.master);
      o.start(t);
      o.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
    } catch (e) {
    }
  }
  click() {
    this.note(880, 0, 0.06, 0.22, "triangle");
  }
  correct() {
    [523.25, 659.25, 783.99].forEach((f, i) => this.note(f, i * 0.08, 0.3, 0.42));
  }
  wrong() {
    this.note(330, 0, 0.18, 0.35, "triangle", 262);
    this.note(262, 0.16, 0.26, 0.3, "triangle", 196);
  }
  fanfare() {
    [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5].forEach((f, i) => this.note(f, i * 0.1, 0.45, 0.4));
    this.note(1318.5, 0.45, 0.65, 0.28, "triangle");
  }
};
var SFX = new SoundFX();
if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
  const unlockEvents = ["click", "touchstart", "touchend", "pointerdown", "keydown"];
  const handleUnlock = () => {
    SFX.unlock();
    try {
      if (window.speechSynthesis && !window._voicesLoaded) {
        window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
        window._voicesLoaded = true;
      }
    } catch (e) {
    }
  };
  unlockEvents.forEach((ev) => window.addEventListener(ev, handleUnlock, { passive: true }));
}

// public/js/license-core.js
var DAY = 864e5;
var ALPHA = "ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789";
function generateCode(prefix = "NMR") {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(12);
  crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
  let s = "";
  for (const b of bytes) s += ALPHA[b % ALPHA.length];
  const p = String(prefix || "NMR").toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, "").slice(0, 6) || "NMR";
  return `${p}-${s.slice(0, 4)}-${s.slice(4, 8)}-${s.slice(8, 12)}`;
}
function normalizeCode(input) {
  const ar = "\u0660\u0661\u0662\u0663\u0664\u0665\u0666\u0667\u0668\u0669";
  const s = String(input || "").replace(/[٠-٩]/g, (d) => String(ar.indexOf(d))).toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, "");
  if (s.length < 13) return s;
  const body = s.slice(-12);
  const prefix = s.slice(0, s.length - 12);
  return `${prefix}-${body.slice(0, 4)}-${body.slice(4, 8)}-${body.slice(8, 12)}`;
}
function checkActivatable(rec, now = Date.now()) {
  if (!rec) return { ok: false, reason: "not_found" };
  if (rec.status === "used") return { ok: false, reason: "used" };
  if (rec.status === "revoked") return { ok: false, reason: "revoked" };
  if (rec.status !== "unused") return { ok: false, reason: "invalid" };
  if (rec.valid_from && now < rec.valid_from) return { ok: false, reason: "not_started" };
  if (rec.valid_until && now > rec.valid_until) return { ok: false, reason: "expired" };
  return { ok: true };
}
function licenseExpiry(rec, activatedAt = Date.now()) {
  const d = Number(rec && rec.duration_days);
  if (!d || d <= 0) return null;
  return activatedAt + d * DAY;
}
function codeStatus(rec, now = Date.now()) {
  if (rec.status === "revoked") return "revoked";
  if (rec.status === "used") return rec.license_exp && now > rec.license_exp ? "ended" : "active";
  if (rec.valid_until && now > rec.valid_until) return "expired";
  if (rec.valid_from && now < rec.valid_from) return "scheduled";
  return "unused";
}
var REASONS_AR = {
  not_found: "\u0627\u0644\u0643\u0648\u062F \u063A\u064A\u0631 \u0645\u0648\u062C\u0648\u062F. \u062A\u0623\u0643\u062F \u0645\u0646 \u0643\u062A\u0627\u0628\u062A\u0647 \u0643\u0645\u0627 \u0647\u0648.",
  used: "\u0647\u0630\u0627 \u0627\u0644\u0643\u0648\u062F \u0627\u0633\u062A\u064F\u062E\u062F\u0645 \u0645\u0646 \u0642\u0628\u0644 \u0648\u062A\u0645 \u062D\u0631\u0642\u0647. \u0643\u0644 \u0643\u0648\u062F \u064A\u0639\u0645\u0644 \u0645\u0631\u0629 \u0648\u0627\u062D\u062F\u0629 \u0641\u0642\u0637.",
  revoked: "\u0647\u0630\u0627 \u0627\u0644\u0643\u0648\u062F \u0623\u064F\u0644\u063A\u064A \u0645\u0646 \u0627\u0644\u0625\u062F\u0627\u0631\u0629.",

  expired: "\u0627\u0646\u062A\u0647\u0649 \u0627\u0644\u0648\u0642\u062A \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0633\u0645\u0648\u062D \u0644\u062A\u0641\u0639\u064A\u0644 \u0647\u0630\u0627 \u0627\u0644\u0643\u0648\u062F.",
  invalid: "\u0627\u0644\u0643\u0648\u062F \u063A\u064A\u0631 \u0635\u0627\u0644\u062D.",
  rate_limited: "\u0645\u062D\u0627\u0648\u0644\u0627\u062A \u0643\u062B\u064A\u0631\u0629. \u062D\u0627\u0648\u0644 \u0628\u0639\u062F \u0642\u0644\u064A\u0644.",
  license_ended: "\u0627\u0646\u062A\u0647\u062A \u0645\u062F\u0629 \u0627\u0634\u062A\u0631\u0627\u0643\u0643. \u0627\u0637\u0644\u0628 \u0643\u0648\u062F\u064B\u0627 \u062C\u062F\u064A\u062F\u064B\u0627 \u0644\u0644\u062A\u062C\u062F\u064A\u062F.",
  device: "\u0647\u0630\u0627 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0641\u0639\u064A\u0644 \u0645\u0631\u062A\u0628\u0637 \u0628\u062C\u0647\u0627\u0632 \u0622\u062E\u0631.",
  network: "\u062A\u0639\u0630\u0651\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0627\u062A\u0635\u0627\u0644 \u0628\u0627\u0644\u062E\u0627\u062F\u0645. \u062A\u0623\u0643\u062F \u0645\u0646 \u0627\u0644\u0625\u0646\u062A\u0631\u0646\u062A \u062B\u0645 \u062D\u0627\u0648\u0644 \u0645\u062C\u062F\u062F\u064B\u0627."
};
var enc = new TextEncoder();
var b64u = (buf) => {
  let s = "";
  const a = buf instanceof Uint8Array ? buf : new Uint8Array(buf);
  for (const x of a) s += String.fromCharCode(x);
  return btoa(s).replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
};
var unb64u = (s) => {
  const t = s.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/");
  const bin = atob(t + "===".slice((t.length + 3) % 4));
  return Uint8Array.from(bin, (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
};
async function hmac(secret, data) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", enc.encode(secret), { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" }, false, ["sign"]);
  return new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, enc.encode(data)));
}
async function signToken(payload, secret) {
  const body = b64u(enc.encode(JSON.stringify(payload)));
  return `${body}.${b64u(await hmac(secret, body))}`;
}
async function verifyToken(token, secret, now = Date.now()) {
  if (typeof token !== "string" || !token.includes(".")) return { ok: false, reason: "invalid" };
  const [body, sig] = token.split(".");
  const expect = b64u(await hmac(secret, body));
  if (expect.length !== sig.length) return { ok: false, reason: "invalid" };
  let diff = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < expect.length; i++) diff |= expect.charCodeAt(i) ^ sig.charCodeAt(i);
  if (diff !== 0) return { ok: false, reason: "invalid" };
  let p;
  try {
    p = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(unb64u(body)));
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, reason: "invalid" };
  }
  if (p.e && now > p.e) return { ok: false, reason: "license_ended", payload: p };
  return { ok: true, payload: p };
}
function parseBatch(input) {
  const count = Math.floor(Number(input.count));
  if (!(count >= 1 && count <= 500)) return { ok: false, error: "\u0639\u062F\u062F \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0643\u0648\u0627\u062F \u0628\u064A\u0646 1 \u0648 500" };
  const vf = input.valid_from ? Number(input.valid_from) : null;
  const vu = input.valid_until ? Number(input.valid_until) : null;
  if (vf && vu && vu <= vf) return { ok: false, error: "\u062A\u0627\u0631\u064A\u062E \u0646\u0647\u0627\u064A\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0641\u0639\u064A\u0644 \u064A\u062C\u0628 \u0623\u0646 \u064A\u0643\u0648\u0646 \u0628\u0639\u062F \u0627\u0644\u0628\u062F\u0627\u064A\u0629" };
  const dur = input.duration_days === null || input.duration_days === "" || input.duration_days === void 0 ? null : Math.floor(Number(input.duration_days));
  if (dur !== null && !(dur >= 1 && dur <= 3650)) return { ok: false, error: "\u0645\u062F\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0627\u0634\u062A\u0631\u0627\u0643 \u0628\u064A\u0646 1 \u0648 3650 \u064A\u0648\u0645\u064B\u0627 (\u0623\u0648 \u0628\u0644\u0627 \u062D\u062F)" };
  return {
    ok: true,
    value: {
      count,
      prefix: String(input.prefix || "NMR").slice(0, 6),
      valid_from: vf,
      valid_until: vu,
      duration_days: dur,
      batch: String(input.batch || "").slice(0, 60),
      note: String(input.note || "").slice(0, 200)
    }
  };
}

// public/js/license.js
var CFG = window.NIMR_CONFIG || {};
var BASE = (CFG.apiBase || "").replace(/\/$/, "");
var LK = "nimr_lic";
var DK = "nimr_dev";
var PK = "nimr_pack";
async function post(path, data, key) {
  let res;
  try {
    res = await fetch(BASE + path, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json", ...key ? { authorization: "Bearer " + key } : {} },
      body: JSON.stringify(data || {})
    });
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, reason: "network" };
  }
  const ct = res.headers.get("content-type") || "";
  if (ct.includes("javascript")) return { ok: true, source: await res.text() };
  try {
    return await res.json();
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, reason: res.status === 404 ? "no_server" : "network" };
  }
}
var http = {
  activate: (b) => post("/api/activate", b),
  verify: (b) => post("/api/verify", b),
  pack: (b) => post("/api/pack", b),
  tutor: (b) => post("/api/tutor", b),
  admin: {
    list: async (key) => {
      try {
        const r4 = await fetch(BASE + "/api/admin/codes", { headers: { authorization: "Bearer " + key } });
        return await r4.json();
      } catch {
        return { ok: false, reason: "network" };
      }
    },
    generate: (key, b) => post("/api/admin/codes", b, key),
    action: (key, b) => post("/api/admin/action", b, key)
  }
};
var DB_KEY = "nimr_demo_db";
var SECRET = "nimr-demo-secret-not-for-production-use";
var DEMO_ADMIN = "demo-admin-key";
var db = () => {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(DB_KEY)) || { codes: {} };
  } catch {
    return { codes: {} };
  }
};
var dbSave = (d) => localStorage.setItem(DB_KEY, JSON.stringify(d));
var demoLicense = async (token, deviceId) => {
  const v = await verifyToken(token, SECRET);
  if (!v.ok) return v;
  if (deviceId && v.payload.d !== deviceId) return { ok: false, reason: "device" };
  const rec = db().codes[v.payload.c];
  if (!rec || rec.status !== "used") return { ok: false, reason: "revoked" };
  if (rec.license_exp && Date.now() > rec.license_exp) return { ok: false, reason: "license_ended" };
  return { ok: true, payload: v.payload, exp: rec.license_exp };
};
var demo = {
  activate: async ({ code, deviceId, student }) => {
    const d = db(), c = normalizeCode(code), rec = d.codes[c], now = Date.now();
    const chk = checkActivatable(rec, now);
    if (!chk.ok) return { ok: false, reason: chk.reason };
    const exp = licenseExpiry(rec, now);
    Object.assign(rec, { status: "used", used_at: now, device_id: deviceId, student, license_exp: exp });
    dbSave(d);
    return { ok: true, token: await signToken({ c, d: deviceId, e: exp, i: now }, SECRET), exp, duration_days: rec.duration_days };
  },
  verify: async ({ token, deviceId }) => {
    const v = await demoLicense(token, deviceId);
    return v.ok ? { ok: true, exp: v.exp } : v;
  },
  pack: async ({ token, deviceId }) => {
    const v = await demoLicense(token, deviceId);
    if (!v.ok) return v;
    const src = await (await fetch(new URL("./pack.demo.js", import.meta.url))).text();
    return { ok: true, source: src };
  },
  tutor: async () => ({ ok: false, reason: "ai_disabled" }),
  admin: {
    list: async (key) => {
      if (key !== DEMO_ADMIN) return { ok: false, reason: "unauthorized" };
      const now = Date.now();
      const rows = Object.values(db().codes).sort((a, b) => b.created_at - a.created_at).map((r4) => ({ ...r4, state: codeStatus(r4, now) }));
      const stats = rows.reduce((a, r4) => (a[r4.state] = (a[r4.state] || 0) + 1, a), { total: rows.length });
      return { ok: true, rows, stats };
    },
    generate: async (key, b) => {
      if (key !== DEMO_ADMIN) return { ok: false, reason: "unauthorized" };
      const p = parseBatch(b);
      if (!p.ok) return { ok: false, error: p.error };
      const d = db(), now = Date.now(), v = p.value;
      const codes = Array.from({ length: v.count }, () => generateCode(v.prefix));
      codes.forEach((c) => d.codes[c] = { code: c, status: "unused", created_at: now, valid_from: v.valid_from, valid_until: v.valid_until, duration_days: v.duration_days, batch: v.batch, note: v.note });
      dbSave(d);
      return { ok: true, codes, ...v };
    },
    action: async (key, b) => {
      if (key !== DEMO_ADMIN) return { ok: false, reason: "unauthorized" };
      const d = db(), c = normalizeCode(b.code), rec = d.codes[c], now = Date.now();
      if (!rec) return { ok: false, reason: "not_found" };
      if (b.action === "revoke") Object.assign(rec, { status: "revoked", revoked_at: now });
      else if (b.action === "delete") {
        if (rec.status === "used") return { ok: false, reason: "in_use" };
        delete d.codes[c];
      } else if (b.action === "extend") {
        const days = Math.floor(Number(b.days));
        if (!(days >= 1)) return { ok: false, reason: "bad_days" };
        if (rec.status === "used") {
          if (rec.license_exp) rec.license_exp = Math.max(now, rec.license_exp) + days * DAY;
        } else rec.duration_days = (rec.duration_days || 0) + days;
      } else if (b.action === "replace") {
        const remaining = rec.status === "used" ? rec.license_exp ? Math.max(1, Math.ceil((rec.license_exp - now) / DAY)) : null : rec.duration_days;
        const nc = generateCode(c.split("-")[0]);
        Object.assign(rec, { status: "revoked", revoked_at: now, note: (rec.note || "") + " \u2192 \u0627\u0633\u062A\u064F\u0628\u062F\u0644 \u0628\u0640 " + nc });
        d.codes[nc] = { code: nc, status: "unused", created_at: now, valid_from: null, valid_until: now + 30 * DAY, duration_days: remaining, batch: rec.batch, note: "\u0628\u062F\u064A\u0644 \u0639\u0646 " + c };
        dbSave(d);
        return { ok: true, code: nc, duration_days: remaining };
      } else return { ok: false, reason: "bad_action" };
      dbSave(d);
      return { ok: true };
    }
  }
};
var sbApi = {
  activate: async ({ code, deviceId, student }) => {
    if (!CFG.supabaseUrl || !CFG.supabaseAnonKey) return demo.activate({ code, deviceId, student });
    try {
      const res = await fetch(CFG.supabaseUrl.replace(/\/$/, "") + "/rest/v1/rpc/activate_code", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "apikey": CFG.supabaseAnonKey,
          "Authorization": "Bearer " + CFG.supabaseAnonKey,
          "Content-Type": "application/json"
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          p_code: code,
          p_device_id: deviceId,
          p_student: student || ""
        })
      });
      if (!res.ok) return { ok: false, reason: "network" };
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!data.ok) return data;
      const token = await signToken({ c: data.code, d: deviceId, e: data.exp, i: Date.now() }, SECRET);
      return { ok: true, token, exp: data.exp, duration_days: data.duration_days };
    } catch {
      return { ok: false, reason: "network" };
    }
  },
  verify: async ({ token, deviceId }) => {
    if (!CFG.supabaseUrl || !CFG.supabaseAnonKey) return demo.verify({ token, deviceId });
    const v = await verifyToken(token, SECRET);
    if (!v.ok) return v;
    if (deviceId && v.payload.d !== deviceId) return { ok: false, reason: "device" };
    try {
      const res = await fetch(CFG.supabaseUrl.replace(/\/$/, "") + "/rest/v1/rpc/verify_code", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "apikey": CFG.supabaseAnonKey,
          "Authorization": "Bearer " + CFG.supabaseAnonKey,
          "Content-Type": "application/json"
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          p_code: v.payload.c,
          p_device_id: deviceId
        })
      });
      if (!res.ok) return { ok: false, reason: "network" };
      const data = await res.json();
      return data;
    } catch {
      return { ok: false, reason: "network" };
    }
  },
  pack: async (b) => demo.pack(b),
  tutor: async () => ({ ok: false, reason: "ai_disabled" }),
  admin: demo.admin
};
var api = (CFG.supabaseUrl && CFG.supabaseAnonKey) ? sbApi : (CFG.demo ? demo : http);
var DEMO_ADMIN_KEY = CFG.demo ? DEMO_ADMIN : "";
var LIC = {
  deviceId() {
    let d = localStorage.getItem(DK);
    if (!d) {
      d = (crypto.randomUUID ? crypto.randomUUID() : String(Math.random()).slice(2) + Date.now()).replace(/-/g, "");
      localStorage.setItem(DK, d);
    }
    return d;
  },
  get() {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LK)) || null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  },
  clear() {
    localStorage.removeItem(LK);
    localStorage.removeItem(PK);
  },
  active() {
    const l = this.get();
    return !!(l && l.token && (!l.exp || Date.now() < l.exp));
  },
  daysLeft() {
    const l = this.get();
    if (!l || !l.exp) return null;
    return Math.max(0, Math.ceil((l.exp - Date.now()) / DAY));
  },
  async activate(code, student) {
    const r4 = await api.activate({ code: normalizeCode(code), deviceId: this.deviceId(), student });
    if (r4.ok) localStorage.setItem(LK, JSON.stringify({ token: r4.token, exp: r4.exp, code: normalizeCode(code), at: Date.now() }));
    return r4;
  },
  /** Online check — clears the licence if the server revoked/ended it. Offline keeps working until expiry. */
  async refresh() {
    const l = this.get();
    if (!l) return { ok: false };
    const r4 = await api.verify({ token: l.token, deviceId: this.deviceId() });
    if (!r4.ok && ["revoked", "license_ended", "invalid", "device"].includes(r4.reason)) this.clear();
    else if (r4.ok && r4.exp !== l.exp) localStorage.setItem(LK, JSON.stringify({ ...l, exp: r4.exp }));
    return r4;
  },
  /** Loads premium lessons into the engine. Uses a cached copy when offline. */
  async loadPack(N2) {
    if (!this.active()) return false;
    const l = this.get();
    let src = null;
    const r4 = await api.pack({ token: l.token, deviceId: this.deviceId() });
    if (r4.ok && r4.source) {
      src = r4.source;
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(PK, src);
      } catch {
      }
    } else if (r4.reason === "network" || r4.reason === "no_server") src = localStorage.getItem(PK);
    else if (["revoked", "license_ended", "invalid", "device"].includes(r4.reason)) this.clear();
    if (!src) return false;
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([src], { type: "text/javascript" }));
    const mod = await import(
      /* @vite-ignore */
      url
    );
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    mod.default(N2);
    return true;
  },
  async tutor(payload) {
    const l = this.get();
    if (!l) return null;
    const r4 = await api.tutor({ ...payload, token: l.token, deviceId: this.deviceId() });
    return r4.ok ? r4.text : null;
  }
};

function ensureSession() {
  if (P.sess && P.sess.d === today()) return;
  const t = [];
  const fl = N.FLUENCY.filter((k) => avail(k) && !locked(k));
  const warm = fl.filter((k) => P.m[k]).pop() || fl[0];
  if (warm && !P.calm) t.push({ t: "warm", s: warm });
  dueList().slice(0, 2).forEach((s) => t.push({ t: "review", s }));
  const nx = nextSkill();
  if (nx) t.push({ t: "learn", s: nx });
  t.push({ t: "hunt" });
  P.sess = { d: today(), t: t.map((x) => ({ ...x, done: 0 })), fin: 0 };
  save();
}
function markTask(type, s) {
  if (!P.sess || P.sess.d !== today()) return;
  const t = P.sess.t.find((x) => !x.done && x.t === type && (!s || !x.s || x.s === s));
  if (!t) return;
  t.done = 1;
  P.xp += 10;
  if (!P.sess.fin && P.sess.t.every((x) => x.done)) {
    P.sess.fin = 1;
    P.xp += 40;
    setTimeout(() => {
      conf();
      toast("\u{1F389} \u0623\u0646\u0647\u064A\u062A \u062C\u0644\u0633\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u064A\u0648\u0645! +40 \u0646\u0642\u0637\u0629");
    }, 300);
  }
  save();
}

async function doLogin() {
  SFX.correct();
  const n = document.getElementById("nm").value.trim(), cEl = document.getElementById("cd2"), c = cEl ? cEl.value.trim() : "", e = document.getElementById("er");
  if (!n) return e.textContent = "\u0627\u0643\u062A\u0628 \u0627\u0633\u0645\u0643 \u0623\u0648\u0644\u064B\u0627";
  if (c) {
    e.textContent = "\u062C\u0627\u0631\u064D \u0627\u0644\u062A\u062D\u0642\u0642 \u0645\u0646 \u0627\u0644\u0643\u0648\u062F\u2026";
    const res = await LIC.activate(c, n);
    if (!res.ok) return e.textContent = REASONS_AR[res.reason] || res.error || "\u062A\u0639\u0630\u0651\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0641\u0639\u064A\u0644";
    await LIC.loadPack(N);
  }
  const S = students();
  if (P.nm && P.nm !== n) S[P.nm] = P;
  P = S[n] ? Object.assign(blank(), S[n]) : Object.assign(blank(), { nm: n });
  P.nm = n;
  P.gr = LG;
  S[n] = P;
  localStorage.setItem(KS, JSON.stringify(S));
  save();
  if (c) toast("\u062A\u0645 \u062A\u0641\u0639\u064A\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0643\u0648\u0631\u0633 \u0627\u0644\u0643\u0627\u0645\u0644! \u0627\u0644\u0643\u0648\u062F \u0627\u062D\u062A\u0631\u0642 \u0648\u0644\u0627 \u064A\u0639\u0645\u0644 \u0645\u0631\u0629 \u0623\u062E\u0631\u0649.");
  home();
}
function switchTo(n) {
  const S = students();
  if (P.nm) S[P.nm] = P;
  P = Object.assign(blank(), S[n] || { nm: n });
  LG = P.gr;
  localStorage.setItem(KS, JSON.stringify(S));
  save();
  home();
}
function home() {
  if (!P.nm) return login();
  V = "home";
  ensureSession();

function area(k) {
  V = "area";
  const a = N.AREAS.find((x) => x.k === k);
  render(hdr() + `<section class="card"><h1>${ic(a.ic)} ${a.n}</h1>${a.sk.map((s) => `<div class="row"><span>${N.SK[s].n} <small class="mut">\u0627\u0644\u0635\u0641 ${N.SK[s].g}</small></span><button class="sm ${P.m[s] ? "" : "pri"}" data-a="menu" data-k="${s}">${locked(s) ? ic("lock") : P.m[s] ? ic("check") + " \u0645\u0631\u0627\u062C\u0639\u0629" : "\u0627\u0628\u062F\u0623"}</button></div>`).join("")}</section>`);
}
function gate(s) {
  if (locked(s)) {
    unlock(s);
    return 1;
  }
  if (!avail(s)) {
    V = "wait";
    render(hdr() + `<section class="card center">${tiger("think", "tg xl")}<h2>\u0646\u062C\u0647\u0651\u0632 \u0627\u0644\u062F\u0631\u0648\u0633\u2026</h2><p class="mut">\u0646\u062D\u062A\u0627\u062C \u0627\u062A\u0635\u0627\u0644\u064B\u0627 \u0628\u0627\u0644\u0625\u0646\u062A\u0631\u0646\u062A \u0645\u0631\u0629 \u0648\u0627\u062D\u062F\u0629 \u0644\u062A\u0646\u0632\u064A\u0644 \u062F\u0631\u0648\u0633 \u0627\u0644\u0643\u0648\u0631\u0633 \u0627\u0644\u0643\u0627\u0645\u0644.</p><button class="btn" data-a="retry" data-k="${s}">\u062D\u0627\u0648\u0644 \u0645\u062C\u062F\u062F\u064B\u0627</button></section>`);
    return 1;
  }
  return 0;
}
function menu(s) {
  if (gate(s)) return;
  V = "menu";
  const sk = N.SK[s], last = P.er[s] && N.ER[P.er[s]];
  render(hdr() + `<section class="card"><h1>${icn(s)} ${sk.n}</h1><p class="mut">${P.m[s] ? `${ic("check")} \u0645\u062A\u0642\u0646\u0629 \xB7 \u0635\u0646\u062F\u0648\u0642 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0631\u0627\u062C\u0639\u0629 ${P.box[s] || 1}/5` : "\u0642\u064A\u062F \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0639\u0644\u0645"}${P.b[s] ? ` \xB7 \u0623\u0641\u0636\u0644 \u062A\u062D\u062F\u064D\u0651: ${P.b[s]}` : ""}</p>
  ${sk.pre.length ? `<p class="mut small">\u062A\u0628\u0646\u064A \u0639\u0644\u0649: ${sk.pre.map((p) => `${N.SK[p].n}${P.m[p] ? " \u2713" : ""}`).join("\u060C ")}</p>` : ""}
  ${last ? `<div class="hint">\u0622\u062E\u0631 \u0641\u0643\u0631\u0629 \u0646\u0639\u0645\u0644 \u0639\u0644\u064A\u0647\u0627: ${N.erc(P.er[s])}</div>` : ""}
  <button class="btn" data-a="learn" data-k="${s}">${ic("book")} \u0627\u0641\u0647\u0645 \u0648\u062A\u062F\u0631\u0651\u0628</button>
  <button class="btn ghost" data-a="prac0" data-k="${s}">${ic("edit")} \u062A\u062F\u0631\u0651\u0628 \u0645\u0628\u0627\u0634\u0631\u0629</button>
  <button class="btn ghost" data-a="hunt" data-k="${s}">${ic("bug")} \u0627\u0643\u062A\u0634\u0641 \u062E\u0637\u0623 \u0646\u0645\u0631 \u0641\u064A \u0647\u0630\u0647 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0647\u0627\u0631\u0629</button>
  ${N.FLUENCY.includes(s) ? `<button class="btn ghost" data-a="game" data-k="${s}">${ic("timer")} ${P.calm ? "\u062A\u062D\u062F\u064A 15 \u0633\u0624\u0627\u0644\u064B\u0627 (\u0627\u0644\u0648\u0636\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u0647\u0627\u062F\u0626)" : "\u062A\u062D\u062F\u064A 60 \u062B\u0627\u0646\u064A\u0629"}</button>` : ""}
  <button class="btn" data-a="quiz" data-k="${s}">${ic("trophy")} \u0627\u062E\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0625\u062A\u0642\u0627\u0646 (5 \u0623\u0633\u0626\u0644\u0629)</button>
  ${P.m[s] ? `<button class="btn ghost" data-a="cert" data-k="${s}">${ic("award")} \u0634\u0647\u0627\u062F\u0629 \u0625\u062A\u0642\u0627\u0646</button>` : ""}</section>`);
}
function learn(s) {
  if (gate(s)) return;
  V = "learn";
  L = { s, w: N.C[s].w.init(), ls: 0, streak: 0, miss: 0 };
  lesson();
  if (P.snd !== 0) setTimeout(() => N.voice() && narrate("w"), 300);
}
function lesson() {
  V = "learn";
  const c = N.C[L.s], sk = N.SK[L.s], { h, cap: cap2 } = c.w.view(L.w), ln = String(c.we).split("<br>").filter((x) => x.trim()), k = Math.min(L.ls, ln.length);
  L.ln = ln;
  render(
    hdr() + `<div class="crumbs"><span class="on">\u2460 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0633</span><span class="${k ? "on" : ""}">\u2461 \u0634\u0627\u0647\u062F</span><span class="${k >= ln.length ? "on" : ""}">\u2462 \u0637\u0631\u064A\u0642\u0629 \u0623\u062E\u0631\u0649</span><span>\u2463 \u062A\u062F\u0631\u0651\u0628</span></div>
  <section class="card"><h2>${ic("sparkle")} \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0633 \u0648\u062C\u0631\u0651\u0628: ${sk.n}</h2><div class="widget">${h}</div><div class="cp">${cap2}</div><button class="sm ghost" data-a="nar" data-k="w">${ic("sound")} \u0627\u0633\u0645\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u0634\u0631\u062D</button></section>
  <section class="card"><div class="rule">${ic("bulb")} <span>${fr2(sk.rem)}</span></div>
  <h2>${ic("edit")} \u0645\u062B\u0627\u0644 \u0645\u062D\u0644\u0648\u0644 \u062E\u0637\u0648\u0629 \u0628\u062E\u0637\u0648\u0629</h2><div class="steps">${ln.slice(0, k).map((x, i) => `<div class="stx${i === k - 1 ? " cur" : ""}" data-a="nar" data-k="l${i}">${fr2(x)}</div>`).join("")}</div>
  ${k < ln.length ? `<button class="btn" data-a="lnx">${k ? "\u0627\u0644\u062E\u0637\u0648\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0627\u0644\u064A\u0629" : "\u0627\u0628\u062F\u0623 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062B\u0627\u0644"}</button>` : `<div class="alt">${tiger("happy", "tg")}<div><b>\u0637\u0631\u064A\u0642\u0629 \u062B\u0627\u0646\u064A\u0629 \u0644\u0644\u0641\u0647\u0645</b><p>${fr2(sk.alt)}</p></div></div><button class="btn big" data-a="prac">\u062C\u0627\u0647\u0632! \u062F\u0639\u0646\u064A \u0623\u062A\u062F\u0631\u0651\u0628 \u2190</button>`}</section>`
  );
}
function narrate(k) {
  if (k === "w") {
    const c = $.querySelector(".cp");
    N.speak((c ? c.innerHTML : "") + ". " + N.SK[L.s].rem);
  } else N.speak(L.ln[+k.slice(1)]);
}
function wact(a, k) {
  const c = N.C[L.s].w, w = L.w;
  if (a === "set") {
    const [key, dl, mn, mx] = k.split(":"), old = w[key];
    w[key] = Math.max(+mn, Math.min(+mx, old + +dl));
    if (c.fix) c.fix(w, key, old);
  } else if (c.on) c.on(a, k, w);
  lesson();
}
function prac(s) {
  if (s) {
    if (gate(s)) return;
    L = { s, streak: 0, miss: 0, w: N.C[s].w.init() };
  }
  V = "prac";
  Object.assign(L, { q: N.gen(L.s, true), show: 0, dead: [], first: true, fb: "", alt: "", guess: 0 });
  pr();
}
function pr() {
  const q = L.q;
  render(
    hdr() + `<section class="card"><div class="top"><span class="mut small">\u0623\u062A\u0642\u0646 \u0628\u0640 3 \u0625\u062C\u0627\u0628\u0627\u062A \u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D\u0629 \u0645\u062A\u062A\u0627\u0644\u064A\u0629 \u0628\u062B\u0642\u0629</span><span class="dots" aria-label="\u0627\u0644\u0633\u0644\u0633\u0644\u0629 ${L.streak} \u0645\u0646 3">${[0, 1, 2].map((i) => `<i class="${i < L.streak ? "on" : ""}"></i>`).join("")}</span></div>
  <div class="q">${fr2(q.t)}</div>
  <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:6px 0;">
    ${listenBtn()}
    <button class="sm ghost" data-a="scratchpad" type="button">${ic("edit")} \u270F\uFE0F \u0645\u0633\u0648\u062F\u0629 \u0646\u0645\u0631 (\u062E\u0631\u0628\u0634 \u0648\u062C\u0631\u0651\u0628)</button>
  </div>
  <div id="scratch-box" style="display:none;"></div>
  ${qbar(q)}
  ${L.show ? "" : `<div class="conf" role="group" aria-label="\u0645\u062F\u0649 \u0627\u0644\u062B\u0642\u0629"><span class="mut small">\u0642\u0628\u0644 \u0623\u0646 \u062A\u062E\u062A\u0627\u0631:</span><button class="chip${L.guess ? "" : " sel"}" data-a="conf" data-k="0">${ic("check")} \u0645\u062A\u0623\u0643\u062F</button><button class="chip${L.guess ? " sel" : ""}" data-a="conf" data-k="1">\u{1F914} \u0623\u062E\u0645\u0651\u0646</button></div>${opts(q, "ans", L.dead)}${idk("ans")}`}
  ${L.fb}${L.alt}<button class="sm ghost" data-a="learn" data-k="${L.s}">${ic("book")} \u0623\u0639\u062F \u0627\u0644\u0634\u0631\u062D</button></section>`
  );
}
function contrast(txt, ok) {
  const pf = (s) => {
    const m2 = String(s).match(/^(\d+)\/(\d+)$/);
    return m2 && +m2[2] <= 40 ? [+m2[1], +m2[2]] : null;
  };
  const m = String(L.q.t).match(/(\d+)\/(\d+)\s*=\s*؟\/(\d+)/);
  if (m && /^\d+$/.test(txt)) return `<div class="hint">\u0627\u0644\u0643\u0633\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0635\u0644\u064A${N.bar(+m[2], (i) => i < +m[1] ? "on" : "")}\u0643\u0633\u0631\u0643 ${fr2(txt + "/" + m[3])}${N.bar(+m[3], (i) => i < Math.min(+txt, +m[3]) ? "on2" : "")}\u0627\u0644\u062C\u0632\u0621 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0644\u0648\u0651\u0646 \u0644\u064A\u0633 \u0645\u062A\u0633\u0627\u0648\u064A\u064B\u0627. \u0627\u0644\u0628\u0633\u0637 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D ${ok}.</div>`;
  const a = pf(txt), b = pf(ok);
  if (a && b && a[0] <= a[1]) return `<div class="hint">\u0643\u0633\u0631\u0643 ${fr2(txt)}${N.bar(a[1], (i) => i < a[0] ? "on2" : "")}\u0627\u0644\u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D ${fr2(ok)}${N.bar(b[1], (i) => i < b[0] ? "on" : "")}\u0642\u0627\u0631\u0646 \u0627\u0644\u062C\u0632\u0621 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0644\u0648\u0651\u0646 \u0641\u064A \u0627\u0644\u0634\u0631\u064A\u0637\u064A\u0646.</div>`;
  const x = N.val(txt), y = N.val(ok);
  if (L.s === "F" && x != null && y != null && /\./.test(txt)) return `<div class="hint">\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0631\u062A\u064E ${txt} (\u0628\u0631\u062A\u0642\u0627\u0644\u064A) \u0648\u0627\u0644\u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D ${ok} (\u0623\u0632\u0631\u0642)<div class="pair">${N.g100(Math.round(x * 100), "on2")}${N.g100(Math.round(y * 100))}</div></div>`;
  return `<details class="hint"><summary>${ic("edit")} \u0634\u0627\u0647\u062F \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062B\u0627\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062D\u0644\u0648\u0644</summary>${fr2(N.C[L.s].we)}</details>`;
}
function ans(t, txt) {
  const s = L.s;
  if (t === "0") {
    SFX.correct();
    if (L.guess) {
      P.gs.n++;
      P.gs.c++;
    }
    if (L.first && !L.guess) {
      L.streak++;
      P.xp += 10;
    } else P.xp += L.first ? 5 : 3;
    save();
    if (L.streak >= 3) {
      P.xp += 30;
      L.streak = 0;
      markTask("learn", s);
      save();
      setTimeout(conf, 50);
      V = "streak";
      return render(hdr() + `<section class="card center">${tiger("happy", "tg xl bob")}<h1>\u062B\u0644\u0627\u062B \u0625\u062C\u0627\u0628\u0627\u062A \u0645\u062A\u062A\u0627\u0644\u064A\u0629!</h1><p>+30 \u0646\u0642\u0637\u0629. \u0623\u0646\u062A \u062C\u0627\u0647\u0632 \u0644\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0625\u062A\u0642\u0627\u0646: 5 \u0623\u0633\u0626\u0644\u0629 \u0628\u0644\u0627 \u062A\u0644\u0645\u064A\u062D\u0627\u062A\u060C \u062A\u0643\u062A\u0628 \u0641\u064A\u0647\u0627 \u0627\u0644\u062C\u0648\u0627\u0628 \u0628\u0646\u0641\u0633\u0643\u060C \u0648\u062A\u0646\u062C\u062D \u0628\u0640 4.</p><button class="btn big" data-a="quiz" data-k="${s}">${ic("trophy")} \u0627\u0628\u062F\u0623 \u0627\u062E\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0625\u062A\u0642\u0627\u0646</button><button class="btn ghost" data-a="prac">\u062A\u062F\u0631\u0651\u0628 \u0623\u0643\u062B\u0631</button></section>`);
    }
    L.fb = `<div class="fb good">${ic("check")} ${say()} ${L.guess ? "<br><small>\u0644\u0643\u0646\u0643 \u062E\u0645\u0651\u0646\u062A\u060C \u0641\u0644\u0646 \u0646\u062D\u0633\u0628\u0647\u0627 \u0641\u064A \u0627\u0644\u0633\u0644\u0633\u0644\u0629. \u062C\u0631\u0651\u0628 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0627\u0644\u064A \u0628\u062B\u0642\u0629!</small>" : L.first ? "+10" : "+3"}<button class="btn" data-a="prac">\u0627\u0644\u0633\u0624\u0627\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0627\u0644\u064A</button></div>`;
    L.show = 1;
    return pr();
  }
  SFX.wrong();
  if (L.guess) P.gs.n++;
  L.first = false;
  L.streak = 0;
  L.miss++;
  if (N.ER[t]) {
    P.er[s] = t;
    P.ec[t] = (P.ec[t] || 0) + 1;
    P.et[t] = s;
  }
  save();
  const pp = L.miss >= 2 ? N.SK[s].pre.find((k) => !P.m[k] && avail(k)) : null;
  const ok = L.q.ans;
  L.dead.push(txt);
  L.show = 1;
  L.last = { t, txt, ok };
  const socraticBtn = `<button class="btn" style="background:#e65100;color:#fff;width:100%;font-weight:700;margin:8px 0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:8px;" data-a="socratic" data-k="${t || "general"}">${tiger("think", "tg sm")} \u{1F42F} \u0646\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u062D\u0643\u064A\u0645: \u0633\u0627\u0639\u062F\u0646\u064A \u0644\u0623\u0643\u062A\u0634\u0641 \u062E\u0637\u0623\u064A</button><div id="socratic-box"></div>`;
  L.fb = `<div class="fb warn"><div class="fbh">${tiger("think", "tg")}<b>${cheer()}${P.nm ? " \u064A\u0627 " + esc2(P.nm) : ""}</b></div>${N.erc(t) ? `<p>${ic("search")} <b>\u0645\u0644\u0627\u062D\u0638\u0629:</b> ${N.erc(t)}.</p>` : ""}${contrast(t === "idk" ? "" : txt, ok)}<p>\u0627\u0644\u0625\u062C\u0627\u0628\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D\u0629: <b>${fr2(ok)}</b></p><div class="rule">${ic("bulb")} <span>${fr2(N.SK[s].rem)}</span></div>${socraticBtn}
  <button class="btn ghost" data-a="alt">${ic("sparkle")} \u0627\u0634\u0631\u062D \u0644\u064A \u0628\u0637\u0631\u064A\u0642\u0629 \u062B\u0627\u0646\u064A\u0629</button>
  ${pp ? `<button class="btn ghost" data-a="learn" data-k="${pp}">${ic("layers")} \u0646\u0631\u062C\u0639 \u062E\u0637\u0648\u0629 \u0644\u0644\u0623\u0633\u0627\u0633: ${N.SK[pp].n}</button>` : ""}<button class="btn" data-a="prac">\u062C\u0631\u0651\u0628 \u0633\u0624\u0627\u0644\u064B\u0627 \u062C\u062F\u064A\u062F\u064B\u0627</button></div>`;
  pr();
}
function altExplain() {
  SFX.click();
  const s = L.s, lt = L.last || {}, tip = lt.t && N.erc(lt.t) ? `<p class="mut">${ic("search")} \u0627\u0646\u062A\u0628\u0647: ${N.erc(lt.t)}.</p>` : "", rem = N.SK[s].rem ? `<div class="rule">${ic("bulb")} <span>${fr2(N.SK[s].rem)}</span></div>` : "";
  L.alt = `<div class="alt">${tiger("happy", "tg")}<div><b>\u0637\u0631\u064A\u0642\u0629 \u062B\u0627\u0646\u064A\u0629</b><p>${fr2(N.SK[s].alt || N.SK[s].rem || "")}</p>${tip}</div></div>`;
  if (!N.SK[s].alt) L.alt += rem;
  pr();
}
function quiz(s, mode = "mastery") {
  if (gate(s)) return;
  V = "quiz";
  Z = { s, mode, n: mode === "review" ? 3 : 5, i: 0, c: 0, w: [], typed: "" };
  zq();
}
function zq() {
  V = "quiz";
  Z.q = N.gen(Z.s, true);
  Z.typed = "";
  Z.kp = N.typable(Z.q);
  quizView();
}
function quizView() {
  const q = Z.q;
  const pad = `<div class="kp-out" dir="ltr" aria-live="polite">${Z.typed ? fr2(Z.typed) : '<span class="mut">\u0627\u0643\u062A\u0628 \u062C\u0648\u0627\u0628\u0643</span>'}</div><div class="kp" dir="ltr">${["7", "8", "9", "4", "5", "6", "1", "2", "3", "/", "0", "."].map((x) => `<button data-a="kp" data-k="${x}">${x}</button>`).join("")}<button data-a="kp" data-k="del" class="kdel">\u232B</button><button data-a="kp" data-k="ok" class="kok">\u062A\u062D\u0642\u0651\u0642</button></div>`;
  render(
    hdr() + `<div class="prog"><i style="width:${Z.i / Z.n * 100}%"></i></div><section class="card"><div class="top"><span class="mut small">${Z.mode === "review" ? "\u0645\u0631\u0627\u062C\u0639\u0629" : "\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0625\u062A\u0642\u0627\u0646"}: \u0633\u0624\u0627\u0644 ${Z.i + 1} \u0645\u0646 ${Z.n} \xB7 \u0628\u0644\u0627 \u062A\u0644\u0645\u064A\u062D\u0627\u062A</span>${Z.i === 0 ? `<button class="sm ghost" data-a="breathe">${ic("wind")} \u062A\u0646\u0641\u0651\u0633 \u0623\u0648\u0644\u064B\u0627</button>` : ""}</div>
  <div class="q">${fr2(q.t)}</div>
  <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:6px 0;">
    ${listenBtn()}
    <button class="sm ghost" data-a="scratchpad" type="button">${ic("edit")} \u270F\uFE0F \u0645\u0633\u0648\u062F\u0629 \u0646\u0645\u0631 (\u062E\u0631\u0628\u0634 \u0648\u062C\u0631\u0651\u0628)</button>
  </div>
  <div id="scratch-box" style="display:none;"></div>
  ${qbar(q)}${Z.kp ? pad : opts(q, "qa")}${idk("qa")}</section>`
  );
}
function qa(t) {
  if (t === "0") {
    Z.c++;
    SFX.correct();
  } else {
    SFX.wrong();
    Z.w.push(t);
    if (N.ER[t]) {
      P.ec[t] = (P.ec[t] || 0) + 1;
      P.et[t] = Z.s;
      P.er[Z.s] = t;
    }
  }
  Z.i++;
  if (Z.i < Z.n) return zq();
  qend();
}
function kp(k) {
  SFX.click();
  if (k === "del") Z.typed = Z.typed.slice(0, -1);
  else if (k === "ok") {
    if (!Z.typed) return;
    const g = N.gradeTyped(Z.q, Z.typed);
    return qa(g.ok ? "0" : g.tag);
  } else if (Z.typed.length < 9) Z.typed += k;
  quizView();
}
function qend() {
  V = "qend";
  const s = Z.s, need = Z.mode === "review" ? 2 : 4, ok = Z.c >= need, was = P.m[s];
  if (Z.mode === "review") {
    if (ok) {
      P.box[s] = Math.min(5, (P.box[s] || 1) + 1);
      P.due[s] = Date.now() + BOX[P.box[s]] * DAY2;
      P.xp += 20;
    } else {
      delete P.m[s];
      P.box[s] = 0;
      delete P.due[s];
      P.plan = [s, ...(P.plan || []).filter((k) => k !== s)];
    }
    markTask("review", s);
  } else if (ok) {
    P.m[s] = 1;
    P.xp += 50;
    P.box[s] = Math.max(1, P.box[s] || 1);
    P.due[s] = Date.now() + BOX[P.box[s]] * DAY2;
    markTask("learn", s);
  } else if (was) {
    delete P.m[s];
    delete P.due[s];
  }
  save();
  if (ok) setTimeout(conf, 50);
  const tip = Z.w.find((x) => N.ER[x]);
  render(
    hdr() + `<section class="card center">${tiger(ok ? "happy" : "think", "tg xl" + (ok ? " bob" : ""))}<h1>${ok ? Z.mode === "review" ? "\u062B\u0628\u0651\u062A\u0651 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0647\u0627\u0631\u0629!" : "\u0623\u062A\u0642\u0646\u062A \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0647\u0627\u0631\u0629!" : "\u0627\u0642\u062A\u0631\u0628\u062A!"}</h1><p class="score">${Z.c} / ${Z.n}</p>
  <p>${ok ? Z.mode === "review" ? `\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0631\u0627\u062C\u0639\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0642\u0627\u062F\u0645\u0629 \u0628\u0639\u062F ${BOX[P.box[s]]} \u0623\u064A\u0627\u0645.` : "+50 \u0646\u0642\u0637\u0629. \u0633\u062A\u0639\u0648\u062F \u0644\u0643 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0647\u0627\u0631\u0629 \u0644\u0644\u0645\u0631\u0627\u062C\u0639\u0629 \u0628\u0639\u062F \u064A\u0648\u0645\u064A\u0646 \u0644\u062A\u062B\u0628\u064A\u062A\u0647\u0627." : `\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0637\u0644\u0648\u0628 ${need}. ${tip ? "\u0645\u0644\u0627\u062D\u0638\u0629: " + N.erc(tip) + ". " : ""}${was ? "\u0639\u0627\u062F\u062A \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0647\u0627\u0631\u0629 \u0625\u0644\u0649 \xAB\u0642\u064A\u062F \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0639\u0644\u0645\xBB \u0644\u0646\u062B\u0628\u0651\u062A\u0647\u0627 \u0645\u0646 \u062C\u062F\u064A\u062F. " : ""}\u0631\u0627\u062C\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u0634\u0631\u062D \u062B\u0645 \u0623\u0639\u062F \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062D\u0627\u0648\u0644\u0629.`}</p>
  ${ok && Z.mode !== "review" ? `<button class="btn" data-a="cert" data-k="${s}">${ic("award")} \u0634\u0647\u0627\u062F\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0625\u062A\u0642\u0627\u0646</button>` : ""}
  <button class="btn ${ok ? "ghost" : ""}" data-a="${ok ? "home" : "learn"}" data-k="${s}">${ok ? "\u0625\u0644\u0649 \u0627\u0644\u0631\u0626\u064A\u0633\u064A\u0629" : ic("book") + " \u0631\u0627\u062C\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u0634\u0631\u062D"}</button>
  ${ok ? "" : `<button class="btn ghost" data-a="quiz" data-k="${s}">${ic("refresh")} \u0623\u0639\u062F \u0627\u0644\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0628\u0627\u0631</button>`}</section>`
  );
}
function breathe() {
  const o = document.createElement("div");
  o.className = "breath";
  o.innerHTML = `<div class="bc"><div class="ball"></div><p id="bt">\u0634\u0647\u064A\u0642\u2026</p><p class="mut small">3 \u0623\u0646\u0641\u0627\u0633 \u0647\u0627\u062F\u0626\u0629. \u0639\u0642\u0644\u0643 \u064A\u0641\u0643\u0651\u0631 \u0623\u0641\u0636\u0644 \u0648\u0647\u0648 \u0647\u0627\u062F\u0626.</p><button class="sm" data-close>\u062A\u062E\u0637\u064E\u0651</button></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(o);
  let n = 0;
  const iv = setInterval(() => {
    n++;
    const t = o.querySelector("#bt");
    if (t) t.textContent = n % 2 ? "\u0632\u0641\u064A\u0631\u2026" : "\u0634\u0647\u064A\u0642\u2026";
    if (n >= 6) close();
  }, 4e3);
  const close = () => {
    clearInterval(iv);
    o.remove();
  };
  o.onclick = (e) => e.target.closest("[data-close]") && close();
}
function place() {
  V = "place";
  const sk = SKS().filter((k) => inCap(k) && avail(k) && !locked(k));
  const leaves = sk.filter((k) => !sk.some((j) => N.SK[j].pre.includes(k)));
  D = { sk, queue: leaves.sort(byOrder).reverse(), res: {}, total: 0 };
  nextD();
}
function nextD() {
  if (!D.queue.length) return placeResult();
  D.cur = D.queue.shift();
  D.ans = [];
  dq();
}
function dq() {
  V = "place";
  D.q = N.gen(D.cur, false);
  render(hdr() + `<div class="prog"><i style="width:${Math.min(95, D.total * 4)}%"></i></div><section class="card"><p class="mut small">${icn(D.cur)} \u0633\u0624\u0627\u0644 ${D.total + 1} \xB7 \u0644\u0627 \u064A\u0648\u062C\u062F \u0631\u0633\u0648\u0628 \u0647\u0646\u0627. \u0625\u0646 \u0644\u0645 \u062A\u0639\u0631\u0641 \u0627\u062E\u062A\u0631 \xAB\u0644\u0627 \u0623\u0639\u0631\u0641 \u0628\u0639\u062F\xBB\u060C \u0641\u0647\u0630\u0627 \u064A\u0633\u0627\u0639\u062F\u0646\u0627.</p><div class="q">${fr2(D.q.t)}</div>${listenBtn()}${qbar(D.q)}${opts(D.q, "dp")}${idk("dp")}</section>`);
}
function dp(t) {
  D.ans.push(t === "0" ? 0 : t);
  D.total++;
  if (D.ans.length < 2 && !(D.ans[0] === "idk")) return dq();
  const wr = D.ans.filter((a) => a !== 0), c = D.ans.length - wr.length, s = D.cur;
  D.res[s] = { st: c === 2 ? "m" : c === 1 ? "p" : "f", idk: D.ans.includes("idk"), tg: wr.find((x) => N.ER[x]) || null };
  if (D.res[s].tg) {
    P.er[s] = D.res[s].tg;
    P.ec[D.res[s].tg] = (P.ec[D.res[s].tg] || 0) + 1;
    P.et[D.res[s].tg] = s;
  }
  if (c === 2) {
    const mark = (k) => N.SK[k].pre.forEach((p) => {
      if (!D.res[p]) {
        D.res[p] = { st: "m", inferred: 1 };
        mark(p);
      }
    });
    mark(s);
    P.dm[s] = 1;
  } else N.SK[s].pre.forEach((p) => {
    if ((!D.res[p] || D.res[p].inferred) && !D.queue.includes(p) && D.sk.includes(p)) {
      delete D.res[p];
      D.queue.unshift(p);
    }
  });
  save();
  nextD();
}
function placeResult() {
  V = "plr";
  const ks = D.sk;
  Object.entries(D.res).forEach(([k, x]) => x.st === "m" && (P.dm[k] = 1));
  P.plan = ks.filter((k) => D.res[k] && D.res[k].st !== "m").sort(byOrder).slice(0, 8);
  P.sess = null;
  save();
  const LB = { m: ["\u0642\u0648\u064A", "m"], p: ["\u0641\u0647\u0645 \u062C\u0632\u0626\u064A", "p"], f: ["\u0646\u0628\u0646\u064A \u0645\u0646 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0633\u0627\u0633", "f_"] };
  const lockedN = SKS().filter((k) => inCap(k) && locked(k)).length;
  render(
    hdr() + `<section class="card"><h1>${ic("map")} \u062E\u0631\u064A\u0637\u0629 \u0645\u0633\u062A\u0648\u0627\u0643</h1><p class="mut">\u0646\u062A\u064A\u062C\u0629 \u0623\u0648\u0644\u064A\u0629 \u0645\u0646 \u0623\u0633\u0626\u0644\u0629 \u0642\u0644\u064A\u0644\u0629\u060C \u0646\u0624\u0643\u062F\u0647\u0627 \u0623\u062B\u0646\u0627\u0621 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u062F\u0631\u064A\u0628. \u0627\u0644\u062E\u0637\u0629 \u062A\u0628\u062F\u0623 \u0628\u0627\u0644\u062C\u0630\u0648\u0631 \u0623\u0648\u0644\u064B\u0627.</p>
  ${ks.sort(byOrder).map((k) => {
      const x = D.res[k];
      const l = x ? LB[x.st] : ["\u0644\u0645 \u062A\u064F\u0641\u062D\u0635", "n"];
      return `<div class="row"><span>${icn(k)} ${N.SK[k].n}</span><span class="tag ${l[1]}">${l[0]}${x && x.inferred ? " (\u0645\u0633\u062A\u0646\u062A\u062C)" : ""}</span></div>` + (x && x.st !== "m" ? `<div class="hint small">${x.tg ? "\u0633\u0628\u0628 \u0645\u062D\u062A\u0645\u0644: " + N.erc(x.tg) : x.idk ? "\u0627\u062E\u062A\u0631\u062A\u064E \xAB\u0644\u0627 \u0623\u0639\u0631\u0641 \u0628\u0639\u062F\xBB\u060C \u0648\u0647\u0630\u0627 \u0645\u0641\u064A\u062F: \u0646\u0628\u062F\u0623 \u0645\u0646 \u0627\u0644\u0634\u0631\u062D." : "\u0623\u062E\u0637\u0627\u0621 \u0645\u062A\u0646\u0648\u0639\u0629\u060C \u0646\u062A\u062D\u0642\u0642 \u0623\u0643\u062B\u0631 \u0623\u062B\u0646\u0627\u0621 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u062F\u0631\u064A\u0628."}</div>` : "");
    }).join("")}
  ${lockedN ? `<p class="mut small">${ic("lock")} ${lockedN} \u0645\u0647\u0627\u0631\u0627\u062A \u0645\u0646 \u0645\u0633\u062A\u0648\u0627\u0643 \u062A\u064F\u0641\u062D\u0635 \u0641\u064A \u0627\u0644\u0643\u0648\u0631\u0633 \u0627\u0644\u0643\u0627\u0645\u0644.</p>` : ""}</section>
  <button class="btn big" data-a="home">${ic("cal")} \u0627\u0639\u0631\u0636 \u062E\u0637\u062A\u064A \u0648\u062C\u0644\u0633\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u064A\u0648\u0645</button>`
  );
}
function game(s) {
  if (gate(s)) return;
  V = "game";
  clearInterval(Gm.iv);
  Gm = { s, sc: 0, t: 60, n: 0, cmb: 0, w: {}, calm: P.calm };
  Gm.q = N.gen(s, false);
  gq();
  if (Gm.calm) return;
  const id = setInterval(() => {
    if (Gm.iv !== id || V !== "game") return clearInterval(id);
    Gm.t--;
    const e = document.getElementById("tm");
    if (e) e.textContent = Gm.t;
    if (Gm.t <= 0) {
      clearInterval(id);
      gend();
    }
  }, 1e3);
  Gm.iv = id;
}
function gq() {
  render(hdr() + `<section class="card"><div class="top"><span class="pill">${Gm.calm ? `${Gm.n}/15` : `${ic("timer")} <b id="tm">${Gm.t}</b>`}</span><span class="pill">${ic("target")} ${Gm.sc} ${Gm.cmb > 2 ? ic("flame") + "\xD7" + Gm.cmb : ""}</span></div><div class="q">${fr2(Gm.q.t)}</div>${opts(Gm.q, "ga")}</section>`);
}
function ga(t) {
  Gm.n++;
  if (t === "0") {
    SFX.correct();
    Gm.sc++;
    Gm.cmb++;
  } else {
    SFX.wrong();
    Gm.cmb = 0;
    Gm.w[t] = (Gm.w[t] || 0) + 1;
  }
  if (Gm.calm && Gm.n >= 15) return gend();
  Gm.q = N.gen(Gm.s, false);
  gq();
}
function gend() {
  V = "gameend";
  const nb = Gm.sc > (P.b[Gm.s] || 0);
  if (nb) P.b[Gm.s] = Gm.sc;
  P.xp += Gm.sc * 2;
  markTask("warm", Gm.s);
  save();
  const wk = Object.entries(Gm.w).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0];
  render(hdr() + `<section class="card center">${tiger("happy", "tg xl")}<h1>${Gm.calm ? "\u0627\u0646\u062A\u0647\u0649 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u062D\u062F\u064A!" : "\u0627\u0646\u062A\u0647\u0649 \u0627\u0644\u0648\u0642\u062A!"}</h1><p class="score">${Gm.sc}</p><p>\u0625\u062C\u0627\u0628\u0629 \u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D\u0629 \xB7 +${Gm.sc * 2} \u0646\u0642\u0637\u0629 ${nb ? "\xB7 \u0631\u0642\u0645 \u0642\u064A\u0627\u0633\u064A \u062C\u062F\u064A\u062F!" : ""}</p>${wk && N.erc(wk[0]) ? `<div class="hint">\u0623\u0643\u062B\u0631 \u0646\u0645\u0637 \u062E\u0637\u0623: ${N.erc(wk[0])}</div>` : ""}<button class="btn" data-a="game" data-k="${Gm.s}">${ic("refresh")} \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0628 \u0645\u0631\u0629 \u062B\u0627\u0646\u064A\u0629</button><button class="btn ghost" data-a="home">\u0627\u0644\u0631\u0626\u064A\u0633\u064A\u0629</button></section>`);
}
function hunt(s) {
  const pool = s ? [s] : [.../* @__PURE__ */ new Set([...P.plan || [], ...SKS().filter((k) => P.m[k])])].filter((k) => avail(k) && !locked(k));
  const p = pool.length ? pool : N.FREE.filter(avail);
  V = "hunt";
  H = { pool: p, i: 0, n: 5, ok: 0, one: s || null };
  hq();
}
function hq() {
  V = "hunt";
  const s = H.pool[r3(0, H.pool.length - 1)];
  let q;
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    q = N.gen(s, false);
    if (q.o.some((o) => o[1] !== 0 && N.ER[o[1]])) break;
  }
  const wrongs = q.o.filter((o) => o[1] !== 0 && N.ER[o[1]]);
  const right = !wrongs.length || Math.random() < 0.25;
  const w = right ? null : wrongs[r3(0, wrongs.length - 1)];
  const tags = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) N.gen(s, false).o.forEach((o) => o[1] !== 0 && N.ER[o[1]] && w && o[1] !== w[1] && tags.add(o[1]));
  const other = sh2([...tags]).slice(0, 2);
  Object.assign(H, { s, q, right, w, step: "judge", fb: "", why: w ? sh2([w[1], ...other]) : [] });
  hv();
}
var tdesc = (t) => N.ER[t].replace(/^قد /, "");
function hv() {
  const q = H.q, shown = H.right ? q.ans : H.w[0];
  render(
    hdr() + `<div class="prog"><i style="width:${H.i / H.n * 100}%"></i></div><section class="card"><div class="fbh">${tiger("think", "tg")}<b>\u062D\u0644\u0651 \u0646\u0645\u0631 \u0647\u0630\u0627 \u0627\u0644\u0633\u0624\u0627\u0644. \u0647\u0644 \u062D\u0644\u0651\u0647 \u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D\u061F</b></div><p class="mut small">${icn(H.s)} ${N.SK[H.s].n} \xB7 \u062C\u0648\u0644\u0629 ${H.i + 1} \u0645\u0646 ${H.n}</p>
  <div class="q">${fr2(q.t)}</div>${qbar(q)}<div class="nimr-ans">\u062C\u0648\u0627\u0628 \u0646\u0645\u0631: <b>${fr2(shown)}</b></div>
  ${H.step === "judge" ? `<div class="two"><button class="opt" data-a="hj" data-k="y">${ic("check")} \u062D\u0644\u0651\u0647 \u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D</button><button class="opt" data-a="hj" data-k="n">${ic("times")} \u0641\u064A\u0647 \u062E\u0637\u0623</button></div>` : ""}
  ${H.step === "why" ? `<p><b>\u0623\u062D\u0633\u0646\u062A! \u0645\u0627 \u0627\u0644\u062E\u0637\u0623 \u0627\u0644\u0630\u064A \u0648\u0642\u0639 \u0641\u064A\u0647 \u0646\u0645\u0631\u061F</b></p>${H.why.map((t) => `<button class="opt txt" data-a="hw" data-k="${t}">\u0646\u0645\u0631 ${tdesc(t)}</button>`).join("")}` : ""}
  ${H.fb}</section>`
  );
}
function hj(v) {
  const q = H.q;
  if (H.right) {
    if (v === "y") {
      H.ok++;
      SFX.correct();
      P.xp += 10;
      H.fb = `<div class="fb good">${ic("check")} \u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D! \u062D\u0644\u0651 \u0646\u0645\u0631 \u0633\u0644\u064A\u0645 \u0647\u0630\u0647 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0631\u0629.${nextH()}</div>`;
    } else {
      SFX.wrong();
      H.fb = `<div class="fb warn">\u062D\u0644\u0651 \u0646\u0645\u0631 \u0643\u0627\u0646 \u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D\u064B\u0627 \u0647\u0630\u0647 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0631\u0629: ${fr2(q.ans)}. \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062D\u0642\u0642 \u0627\u0644\u062C\u064A\u062F \u064A\u062A\u0623\u0643\u062F \u0642\u0628\u0644 \u0623\u0646 \u064A\u062D\u0643\u0645!${nextH()}</div>`;
    }
    H.step = "done";
  } else if (v === "n") H.step = "why";
  else {
    H.step = "done";
    SFX.wrong();
    H.fb = `<div class="fb warn">\u0627\u0646\u062A\u0628\u0647! \u0646\u0645\u0631 \u0623\u062E\u0637\u0623. \u0627\u0644\u062C\u0648\u0627\u0628 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D ${fr2(q.ans)}.<br>\u062E\u0637\u0624\u0647: ${tdesc(H.w[1])}.${nextH()}</div>`;
  }
  save();
  hv();
}
function hw(t) {
  H.step = "done";
  if (t === H.w[1]) {
    H.ok++;
    SFX.correct();
    P.xp += 15;
    H.fb = `<div class="fb good">${ic("search")} \u0639\u064A\u0646 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u062D\u0642\u0642! \u0646\u0645\u0631 ${tdesc(t)}. \u0627\u0644\u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D: <b>${fr2(H.q.ans)}</b>${nextH()}</div>`;
  } else {
    SFX.wrong();
    H.fb = `<div class="fb warn">\u0642\u0631\u064A\u0628! \u062E\u0637\u0623 \u0646\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u062D\u0642\u064A\u0642\u064A: ${tdesc(H.w[1])}. \u0627\u0644\u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D: <b>${fr2(H.q.ans)}</b>${nextH()}</div>`;
  }
  save();
  hv();
}
var nextH = () => `<button class="btn" data-a="hn">${H.i + 1 < H.n ? "\u0627\u0644\u062C\u0648\u0644\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0627\u0644\u064A\u0629" : "\u0627\u0644\u0646\u062A\u064A\u062C\u0629"}</button>`;
function hn() {
  H.i++;
  if (H.i < H.n) return hq();
  P.hunt.ok += H.ok;
  P.hunt.n += H.n;
  markTask("hunt");
  save();
  if (H.ok >= 4) setTimeout(conf, 50);
  V = "huntend";
  render(hdr() + `<section class="card center">${tiger("happy", "tg xl bob")}<h1>${H.ok >= 4 ? "\u0623\u0646\u062A \u0645\u062D\u0642\u0642 \u0631\u064A\u0627\u0636\u064A\u0627\u062A!" : "\u062A\u062D\u0642\u064A\u0642 \u062C\u064A\u062F!"}</h1><p class="score">${H.ok} / ${H.n}</p><p class="mut">\u0639\u0646\u062F\u0645\u0627 \u062A\u0643\u062A\u0634\u0641 \u062E\u0637\u0623 \u063A\u064A\u0631\u0643\u060C \u064A\u0635\u0639\u0628 \u0623\u0646 \u062A\u0642\u0639 \u0641\u064A\u0647 \u0623\u0646\u062A.</p><button class="btn" data-a="hunt" data-k="${H.one || ""}">${ic("refresh")} \u062C\u0648\u0644\u0629 \u062C\u062F\u064A\u062F\u0629</button><button class="btn ghost" data-a="home">\u0627\u0644\u0631\u0626\u064A\u0633\u064A\u0629</button></section>`);
}
function skillMap() {

}
function why() {
  V = "why";
  const e = Object.entries(P.ec).filter((x) => N.ER[x[0]]).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 6);
  render(hdr() + `<section class="card"><h1>${ic("bulb")} \u0623\u0641\u0643\u0627\u0631\u064A: \u0623\u064A\u0646 \u0623\u062A\u062D\u0633\u0651\u0646\u061F</h1>${e.length ? e.map((x) => `<div class="row"><span>${N.erc(x[0])}</span><span class="tag p">${x[1]}\xD7</span>${P.et[x[0]] && avail(P.et[x[0]]) ? `<button class="sm pri" data-a="learn" data-k="${P.et[x[0]]}">\u0639\u0627\u0644\u062C\u0647\u0627</button>` : ""}</div>`).join("") + '<p class="mut small">\u0647\u0630\u0647 \u0623\u0641\u0643\u0627\u0631 \u0646\u0639\u0645\u0644 \u0639\u0644\u064A\u0647\u0627 \u0627\u0644\u0622\u0646\u060C \u0648\u0647\u064A \u0637\u0628\u064A\u0639\u064A\u0629 \u0639\u0646\u062F \u0643\u0644 \u0645\u062A\u0639\u0644\u0651\u0645. \u0643\u0644 \u0641\u0643\u0631\u0629 \u062A\u062E\u062A\u0641\u064A \u062D\u064A\u0646 \u0646\u0641\u0647\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u0633\u0628\u0628.</p>' : "<p>\u0644\u0645 \u0646\u0633\u062C\u0651\u0644 \u0623\u0641\u0643\u0627\u0631\u064B\u0627 \u062E\u0627\u0637\u0626\u0629 \u0628\u0639\u062F. \u062A\u062F\u0631\u0651\u0628 \u062B\u0645 \u0627\u0631\u062C\u0639 \u0647\u0646\u0627.</p>"}</section>`);
}
function report() {
  V = "report";
  const ks = SKS(), mk = ks.filter((k) => P.m[k]), days = [...Array(7)].map((_, i) => {
    const d = new Date(Date.now() - (6 - i) * DAY2);
    return [d.toLocaleDateString("ar", { weekday: "short" }), P.min[d.toISOString().slice(0, 10)] || 0];
  }), mx = Math.max(15, ...days.map((d) => d[1])), tot = Math.round(days.reduce((a, d) => a + d[1], 0));
  const ec = Object.entries(P.ec).filter((x) => N.ER[x[0]]).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 4);
  const gr = P.gs.n ? Math.round(P.gs.n / Math.max(1, P.gs.n + Object.values(P.ec).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)) * 100) : 0;
  const rec = [];
  if (tot < 60) rec.push("\u0627\u0644\u0647\u062F\u0641 15 \u062F\u0642\u064A\u0642\u0629 \u064A\u0648\u0645\u064A\u064B\u0627\u060C 5 \u0623\u064A\u0627\u0645 \u0641\u064A \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0633\u0628\u0648\u0639. \u0627\u0644\u0627\u0646\u062A\u0638\u0627\u0645 \u0623\u0647\u0645 \u0645\u0646 \u0637\u0648\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u062C\u0644\u0633\u0629.");
  if (ec[0]) rec.push(`\u0631\u0643\u0651\u0632\u0648\u0627 \u0647\u0630\u0627 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0633\u0628\u0648\u0639 \u0639\u0644\u0649: \xAB${N.ER[ec[0][0]].replace(/^قد /, "")}\xBB. \u0627\u0637\u0644\u0628\u0648\u0627 \u0645\u0646\u0647 \u0623\u0646 \u064A\u0634\u0631\u062D \u0644\u0643\u0645 \u0628\u0635\u0648\u062A \u0639\u0627\u0644\u064D \u0643\u064A\u0641 \u0641\u0643\u0651\u0631.`);
  if (dueList().length) rec.push(`\u0644\u062F\u064A\u0647 ${dueList().length} \u0645\u0647\u0627\u0631\u0627\u062A \u062A\u0646\u062A\u0638\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0631\u0627\u062C\u0639\u0629\u061B \u0627\u0644\u0645\u0631\u0627\u062C\u0639\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u0642\u0635\u064A\u0631\u0629 \u062A\u0645\u0646\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u0646\u0633\u064A\u0627\u0646.`);
  if (gr > 30) rec.push("\u064A\u062E\u0645\u0651\u0646 \u0643\u062B\u064A\u0631\u064B\u0627. \u0634\u062C\u0651\u0639\u0648\u0647 \u0639\u0644\u0649 \u0627\u062E\u062A\u064A\u0627\u0631 \xAB\u0644\u0627 \u0623\u0639\u0631\u0641 \u0628\u0639\u062F\xBB \u0628\u062F\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u062E\u0645\u064A\u0646\u061B \u0647\u0630\u0627 \u064A\u062C\u0639\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u062E\u0637\u0629 \u0623\u062F\u0642.");
  rec.push("\u0627\u0645\u062F\u062D\u0648\u0627 \u0627\u0644\u062C\u0647\u062F \u0648\u0627\u0644\u0637\u0631\u064A\u0642\u0629 (\xAB\u0641\u0643\u0651\u0631\u062A \u062C\u064A\u062F\u064B\u0627\xBB) \u0623\u0643\u062B\u0631 \u0645\u0646 \u0627\u0644\u0630\u0643\u0627\u0621 (\xAB\u0623\u0646\u062A \u0630\u0643\u064A\xBB).");
  const txt = `\u{1F4CA} \u062A\u0642\u0631\u064A\u0631 ${CFG2.brand || "\u0646\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0631\u064A\u0627\u0636\u064A\u0627\u062A"} \u2014 ${P.nm}
\u2705 \u0627\u0644\u0625\u062A\u0642\u0627\u0646: ${mk.length}/${ks.length} \u0645\u0647\u0627\u0631\u0629 (${pct()}%)
\u23F1 \u0647\u0630\u0627 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0633\u0628\u0648\u0639: ${tot} \u062F\u0642\u064A\u0642\u0629 \xB7 \u{1F525} ${P.sd || 0} \u064A\u0648\u0645 \u0645\u062A\u062A\u0627\u0644\u064A
\u{1F9F1} \u0646\u0639\u0645\u0644 \u0639\u0644\u0649: ${(P.plan || []).filter((k) => !P.m[k]).map((k) => N.SK[k].n).join("\u060C ") || "\u2014"}
\u{1F4A1} ${rec[0]}`;
  render(
    hdr() + `<section class="card report"><div class="rep-h">${tiger("happy", "tg xl")}<div><h1>\u062A\u0642\u0631\u064A\u0631 \u0648\u0644\u064A \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0645\u0631</h1><p><b>${esc2(P.nm)}</b> \xB7 \u0627\u0644\u0635\u0641 ${esc2(P.gr)} \xB7 ${(/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toLocaleDateString("ar")}</p></div><div class="ring" style="--p:${pct()}"><b>${pct()}%</b><small>\u0625\u062A\u0642\u0627\u0646</small></div></div>
  <h2>\u0627\u0644\u062A\u0642\u062F\u0645 \u062D\u0633\u0628 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0628\u0648\u0627\u0628</h2>${N.AREAS.map((a) => {
      const m = a.sk.filter((k) => P.m[k]).length;
      return `<div class="arow"><span>${ic(a.ic)} ${a.n}</span><i class="meter"><i style="width:${m / a.sk.length * 100}%"></i></i><small>${m}/${a.sk.length}</small></div>`;
    }).join("")}
  <h2>\u0648\u0642\u062A \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0639\u0644\u0645 (\u0622\u062E\u0631 7 \u0623\u064A\u0627\u0645): ${tot} \u062F\u0642\u064A\u0642\u0629</h2><div class="week">${days.map((d) => `<div><i style="height:${d[1] / mx * 100}%"></i><small>${d[0]}</small></div>`).join("")}</div>
  <h2>\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0647\u0627\u0631\u0627\u062A</h2><table class="tbl"><tr><th>\u0627\u0644\u0645\u0647\u0627\u0631\u0629</th><th>\u0627\u0644\u062D\u0627\u0644\u0629</th></tr>${ks.sort(byOrder).filter((k) => inCap(k) || P.m[k]).map((k) => `<tr><td>${N.SK[k].n}</td><td><span class="tag ${P.m[k] ? "\u062A\u062D\u062A\u0627\u062C \u0645\u0631\u0627\u062C\u0639\u0629" : "\u0645\u062A\u0642\u0646\u0629" : (P.plan || []).includes(k) ? "\u0642\u064A\u062F \u0627\u0644\u0639\u0644\u0627\u062C" : "\u0644\u0645 \u062A\u0628\u062F\u0623"}</span></td></tr>`).join("")}</table>
  ${ec.length ? `<h2>\u0623\u0643\u062B\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u0641\u0643\u0627\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u062E\u0627\u0637\u0626\u0629 \u062A\u0643\u0631\u0627\u0631\u064B\u0627</h2><ul>${ec.map((x) => `<li>${N.ER[x[0]]} <small class="mut">(${x[1]} \u0645\u0631\u0629)</small></li>`).join("")}</ul>` : ""}
  <h2>\u0645\u0624\u0634\u0631\u0627\u062A</h2><p>\u062A\u062D\u0644\u064A\u0644 \u0627\u0644\u0623\u062E\u0637\u0627\u0621 (\u0627\u0643\u062A\u0634\u0641 \u062E\u0637\u0623 \u0646\u0645\u0631): ${P.hunt.n ? Math.round(P.hunt.ok / P.hunt.n * 100) + "%" : "\u2014"} \xB7 \u0646\u0633\u0628\u0629 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u062E\u0645\u064A\u0646: ${gr}%</p>
  <h2>\u062A\u0648\u0635\u064A\u0627\u062A \u0644\u0644\u0623\u0633\u0628\u0648\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u0642\u0627\u062F\u0645</h2><ol>${rec.map((x) => `<li>${x}</li>`).join("")}</ol></section>
  <div class="noprint"><button class="btn" data-a="prt">${ic("print")} \u0627\u0637\u0628\u0639 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0642\u0631\u064A\u0631 / \u0627\u062D\u0641\u0638\u0647 PDF</button><button class="btn ghost" data-a="wa" data-k="${encodeURIComponent(txt)}">${ic("chat")} \u0623\u0631\u0633\u0644\u0647 \u0639\u0628\u0631 \u0648\u0627\u062A\u0633\u0627\u0628</button></div>`
