async function act() {
  const v = document.getElementById("cd").value, e = document.getElementById("er"), b = $.querySelector('[data-a="act"]');
  if (!v.trim()) return e.textContent = "\u0627\u0643\u062A\u0628 \u0627\u0644\u0643\u0648\u062F \u0623\u0648\u0644\u064B\u0627";
  b.disabled = true;
  e.textContent = "\u062C\u0627\u0631\u064D \u0627\u0644\u062A\u062D\u0642\u0642\u2026";
  const res = await LIC.activate(v, P.nm);
  if (!res.ok) {
    b.disabled = false;
    return e.textContent = REASONS_AR[res.reason] || res.error || "\u062A\u0639\u0630\u0651\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0641\u0639\u064A\u0644";
  }
  await LIC.loadPack(N);
  P.sess = null;
  save();
  conf();
  toast(res.exp ? `\u062A\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0641\u0639\u064A\u0644 \u062D\u062A\u0649 ${new Date(res.exp).toLocaleDateString("ar")}` : "\u062A\u0645 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0641\u0639\u064A\u0644 \u2014 \u0627\u0634\u062A\u0631\u0627\u0643 \u062F\u0627\u0626\u0645");
  home();
}
var currentScratchpad = null;
function toggleScratchpad() {
  const box = document.getElementById("scratch-box");
  if (!box) return;
  if (box.style.display === "none" || !box.innerHTML.trim()) {
    currentScratchpad = new Scratchpad("active-scratchpad");
    box.innerHTML = currentScratchpad.renderUI();
    box.style.display = "block";
    currentScratchpad.init(box);
  } else {
    box.style.display = "none";
  }
}
function openSocraticTutor(tag) {
  const box = document.getElementById("socratic-box");
  if (!box) return;
  const help = SocraticTutor.getHelp(tag, L.q ? L.q.t : "", L.q ? L.q.ans : "");
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="socratic-card">
      <div class="socratic-header">
        ${tiger("think", "tg")}
        <div>
          <div class="socratic-title">\u{1F42F} \u062D\u0648\u0627\u0631 \u0646\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0633\u0642\u0631\u0627\u0637\u064A: ${esc2(help.title)}</div>
          <div class="mut small">${esc2(help.thought)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="socratic-q">${fr2(help.q)}</div>
      <div class="socratic-opts">
        ${help.opts.map((opt, i) => `
          <button type="button" class="socratic-btn" data-soc-i="${i}" data-soc-tag="${tag}">${esc2(opt.text)}</button>
        `).join("")}
      </div>
      <div id="soc-feedback" style="display:none;"></div>
    </div>
  `;
  box.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "nearest" });
}
function handleSocraticAnswer(btn) {
  const i = +btn.dataset.socI;
  const tag = btn.dataset.socTag;
  const help = SocraticTutor.getHelp(tag, L.q ? L.q.t : "", L.q ? L.q.ans : "");
  const opt = help.opts[i];
  const fb = document.getElementById("soc-feedback");
  if (!fb || !opt) return;
  if (opt.right) {
    SFX.correct();
    fb.className = "socratic-fb good";
    fb.innerHTML = `<div><b>${ic("check")} \u0641\u0643\u0631\u0629 \u0630\u0643\u064A\u0629 \u062C\u062F\u064B\u0627 \u0648\u0645\u062A\u0642\u0646\u0629!</b></div><p>${esc2(opt.feedback)}</p><div class="rule">${ic("bulb")} <span>${fr2(help.rule)}</span></div><button class="btn" style="margin-top:10px;" data-a="prac">\u0623\u0646\u0627 \u0645\u0633\u062A\u0639\u062F: \u0633\u0624\u0627\u0644 \u062C\u062F\u064A\u062F!</button>`;
  } else {
    SFX.wrong();
    fb.className = "socratic-fb warn";
    fb.innerHTML = `<div><b>${tiger("think", "tg sm")} \u0645\u0647\u0644\u0627\u064B \u064A\u0627 \u0628\u0637\u0644\u060C \u062A\u0639\u0627\u0644 \u0646\u062A\u0623\u0645\u0644:</b></div><p>${esc2(opt.feedback)}</p><div class="rule">${ic("bulb")} <span>${fr2(help.rule)}</span></div>`;
  }
  fb.style.display = "block";
}
$.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
  const socBtn = e.target.closest("[data-soc-i]");
  if (socBtn) return handleSocraticAnswer(socBtn);
  const t = e.target.closest("[data-a]");
  if (!t || t.disabled) return;
  const a = t.dataset.a, k = t.dataset.k;
  SFX.ready();
  if (!["ans", "qa", "ga", "hj", "hw", "kp", "dp", "alt"].includes(a)) SFX.click();
  const map = {
    home,
    login,
    place,
    map: skillMap,
    why,
    report,
    settings,
    unlock: () => unlock(),
    act,
    breathe,
    area: () => area(k),
    menu: () => menu(k),
    learn: () => learn(k),
    prac: () => prac(),
    prac0: () => prac(k),
    ans: () => ans(k, t.dataset.t),
    alt: altExplain,
    quiz: () => quiz(k),
    review: () => quiz(k, "review"),
    qa: () => qa(k),
    kp: () => kp(k),
    dp: () => dp(k),
    game: () => game(k),
    ga: () => ga(k),
    hunt: () => hunt(k || void 0),
    hj: () => hj(k),
    hw: () => hw(k),
    hn,
    cert: () => cert(k),
    task: () => runTask(+k),
    retry: () => LIC.loadPack(N).then(() => menu(k)),
    conf: () => {
      L.guess = +k;
      pr();
    },
    lnx: () => {
      L.ls++;
      lesson();
      if (P.snd !== 0 && N.voice()) narrate("l" + (L.ls - 1));
    },
    nar: () => N.voice() ? narrate(k) : toast("\u0644\u0627 \u064A\u062A\u0648\u0641\u0631 \u0635\u0648\u062A \u0639\u0631\u0628\u064A \u0639\u0644\u0649 \u0647\u0630\u0627 \u0627\u0644\u062C\u0647\u0627\u0632"),
    rq: readQ,
    scratchpad: toggleScratchpad,
    socratic: () => openSocraticTutor(k),
    fx: () => {
      P.fx = P.fx === 0 ? 1 : 0;
      save();
      if (P.fx !== 0) SFX.correct();
      settings();
    },
    snd: () => {
      P.snd = P.snd === 0 ? 1 : 0;
      N.hush();
      save();
      V === "settings" ? settings() : t.innerHTML = ic(P.snd === 0 ? "mute" : "sound");
    },
    calm: () => {
      P.calm = P.calm ? 0 : 1;
      P.sess = null;
      save();
      settings();
    },
    setgr: () => {
      P.gr = k;
      save();
      settings();
    },
    gr: () => {
      document.querySelectorAll(".chip[data-a=gr]").forEach((c) => c.classList.remove("sel"));
      t.classList.add("sel");
      LG = k;
    },
    pick: () => switchTo(k),
    prt: () => window.print(),
    wa: () => window.open("https://wa.me/?text=" + k, "_blank"),
    buy: () => window.open("https://wa.me/" + CFG2.whatsapp + "?text=" + encodeURIComponent(`\u0623\u0631\u064A\u062F \u0643\u0648\u062F \u062A\u0641\u0639\u064A\u0644 ${CFG2.brand || "\u0646\u0645\u0631 \u0627\u0644\u0631\u064A\u0627\u0636\u064A\u0627\u062A"} \u0644\u0644\u0637\u0627\u0644\u0628 ${P.nm || ""}`), "_blank"),
    out: () => {
      const S = students();
      if (P.nm) S[P.nm] = P;
      localStorage.setItem(KS, JSON.stringify(S));
      P = blank();
      save();
      login();
    },
    exp: () => {
      const c = btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(P))));
      navigator.clipboard ? navigator.clipboard.writeText(c).then(() => toast("\u062A\u0645 \u0646\u0633\u062E \u0631\u0645\u0632 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0642\u062F\u0645"), () => prompt("\u0627\u0646\u0633\u062E \u0627\u0644\u0631\u0645\u0632", c)) : prompt("\u0627\u0646\u0633\u062E \u0627\u0644\u0631\u0645\u0632", c);
    },
    imp: () => {
      const c = prompt("\u0627\u0644\u0635\u0642 \u0631\u0645\u0632 \u0627\u0644\u062A\u0642\u062F\u0645");
      if (!c) return;
      try {
        const o = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(c.trim()))));
        if (!o || !o.m) throw 0;
        P = Object.assign(blank(), o);
        save();
        home();
      } catch {
        toast("\u0627\u0644\u0631\u0645\u0632 \u063A\u064A\u0631 \u0635\u062D\u064A\u062D");
      }
    },
    login: doLogin
  };
  if (map[a]) return map[a]();
  if (V === "learn") wact(a, k);
});
document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
  if (V === "quiz" && Z.kp && /^[0-9/.]$|^Backspace$|^Enter$/.test(e.key)) {
    e.preventDefault();
    kp(e.key === "Backspace" ? "del" : e.key === "Enter" ? "ok" : e.key);
  }
});
setInterval(() => {
  if (document.visibilityState === "visible" && P.nm && !["home", "login", "settings", "report", "map", "lock"].includes(V)) {
    P.min[dkey()] = (P.min[dkey()] || 0) + 0.5;
    save();
  }
}, 3e4);
(async () => {
  if (LIC.active()) {
    $.innerHTML = `<div class="boot">${tiger("happy", "tg xl bob")}<p>\u0646\u062C\u0647\u0651\u0632 \u062F\u0631\u0648\u0633\u0643\u2026</p></div>`;
    await LIC.loadPack(N).catch(() => {
    });
    LIC.refresh().catch(() => {
    });
  }
  home();
})();
if ("serviceWorker" in navigator && !CFG2.demo && location.protocol === "https:") navigator.serviceWorker.register("sw.js").catch(() => {
});
